# Rashi on I Samuel

רש"י על שמואל א

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sefaria vocalized edition (Segments: 639, License: unknown)

Version (En): The Metsudah Tanach series, Lakewood, N.J (Segments: 639)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:1)


**וַיְהִי אִישׁ אֶחָד.** ״הַכֹּל לְפִי הַסֵּדֶר, מֹשֶׁה מָסַר אֶת הַתּוֹרָה לִיהוֹשֻׁעַ, וִיהוֹשֻׁעַ לִזְקֵנִים, וְשׁוֹפֵט מָסַר לְשׁוֹפֵט, עַד שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לְעֵלִי, וּמִמֶּנּוּ לִשְׁמוּאֵל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ (אבות א א): ״וּזְקֵנִים לִנְבִיאִים:


**And there was a man.** It all follows the order: Moshe gave over the Torah to Yehoshua and Yehoshua to the Elders and each Judge to his successor until it reached Eili who gave it over to Shmuel¹ as we learned (in the Mishnah), "… and the Elders [gave it over] to the Prophets…"²

footnotes:
¹ I.e., the “ו” of וְאֵלֶה links the Book of Shmuel with the preceeding Book of Shoftim and serves as a link between the oral tradition as stated in the Mishnah, “…and the Elders…”
² Maseches Avos 1:1.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:2)


**מִן הָרָמָתַיִם צוֹפִים.** שְׁתֵּי רָמוֹת הָיוּ, שֶׁצּוֹפוֹת וְרוֹאוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: צוֹפִים, ״מִתַּלְמִידֵי נְבִיאַיָא״:


**From Ramasayim Tsofim.** There were two hills each visible to the other.³⁴ Targum Yonoson renders צוֹפִים: "of the disciples of prophets."

footnotes:
³ Elkonoh lived in Romoh, see below verse 19; and he prophesied in Tsofim—Da’as Sofrim.
⁴ Maseches Megillah 14a.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:3)


**אֶלְקָנָה.** לֵוִי הָיָה, מִבְּנֵי אֲבִיאָסָף בֶּן קֹרַח, כֵּן נִתְיַחֵס בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים (דברי הימים א ה:ח):


**Elkonoh.** He was a Levite of the sons of Aviosof, the son of Korach —his ancestry is thus recorded in Divrei Hayomim.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ I Divrei Hayomim 6:19-23.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:1:4)


**אֶפְרָתִי.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״בְּטוּרָא דְבֵית אֶפְרַיִם״ (בְּהַר אֶפְרַיִם). וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (ילקוט שמעוני שמואל א רמז עז): **אֶפְרָתִי.** בֶּן פַּלְטִין אַבְגִינוֹס, אָדָם חָשׁוּב, כְּמוֹ (בבא מציעא קיט.): אַפִּרְיוֹן נִמְטְיֵיהּ לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן, לְשׁוֹן חֵן:


**From the land of Ephrayim.** [Targum] Yonoson renders "on the mountain of the house of Ephrayim." The Midrash Aggadah [renders] אֶפְרָתִי —a palace dweller, an important person,⁶ as in "let us extend אַפִּרְיוֹן to Rabbi Shimon,"⁷ [meaning] an expression of favor.

footnotes:
⁶ Rashi emphasizes that אֶפְרָתִי does not mean that he was from the tribe of Ephrayim because it has already been established that he was from the tribe of Leivi.
⁷ Maseches Bava Metzia 119a.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:3:1)


**וְעָלָה הָאִישׁ.** לְשׁוֹן הוֹוֶה הוּא, הָיָה עוֹלֶה מִזְּמַן מוֹעֵד לְמוֹעֵד לְשִׁילֹה. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (ילקוט שמעוני שם): בְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהָיָה עוֹלֶה בְּשָׁנָה זוֹ, לֹא הָיָה עוֹלֶה בְּשָׁנָה אַחֶרֶת, כְּדֵי לְהַשְׁמִיעַ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיַעֲשׂוּ כְמוֹתוֹ:


**This man would go up.** This is the present tense, i.e., he would go up from one appointed season to another, to Shilo.⁸ The Midrash Aggadah [renders] that the route he followed this year he did not take the next year in order to publicize [his pilgrimage] to the Bnei Yisroel that they should do likewise.⁹

footnotes:
⁸ He would go to Shiloh on the three pilgrimage festivals of Pesach, Shavuos and Sukkos.
⁹ See Talmud Yerushalmi, Berachos 9:5.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:4:1)


**וַיְהִי הַיּוֹם.** ״תַּרְגּוּם: ״וַהֲוָה יוֹם מוֹעֲדָא:


**It happened on the day.** It happened on the festive day.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:5:1)


**מָנָה אַחַת אַפָּיִם.** (תרגום:) ״חוּלַק חַד בְּחִיר״, הָרָאוּי לְהִתְקַבֵּל בְּסֵבֶר פָּנִים יָפוֹת:


**One choice portion.** One choice portion which is fit to be accepted with a friendly countenance.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ אַפָּיִם referring to the face rather than to the portion. Ralbag renders “a double sized portion.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:1)


**צָרָתָהּ.** אֵשֶׁת בַּעְלָהּ, פְּנִנָּה:


**Her rival.** Her husband's other wife, Penina.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:2)


**גַּם כַּעַס.** כַּעַס אַחַר כַּעַס, תָּמִיד, לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר ׳גַּם כַּעַס׳, הָיְתָה אוֹמֶרֶת לָהּ: כְּלוּם קָנִית הַיּוֹם מַעֲפוֹרֶת לִבְּנֵךְ גָּדוֹל, אוֹ חָלוּק לִבְּנֵךְ קָטָן:


**Frequently anger.** Anger after anger, i.e., always.' Therefore it is written 'also [גֵם] anger.' She would say to her, "Did you buy your older son a cloak today, or your younger son, a shirt?"


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:3)


**בַּעֲבוּר הַרְעִמָהּ.** שֶׁתִּתְאוֹנֵן, וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (בבא בתרא טז.): בַּעֲבוּר הַרְעִימָהּ, שֶׁתִּתְפַּלֵּל, וְלְשֵׁם שָׁמַיִם נִתְכַּוְּנָה:


**In order that she should complain.** In order to make her complain. Our Rabbis explain, 'in order to make her storm,' that she pray. Penina had good intentions.¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ Maseches Bava Basra 16a.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:6:4)


**בְּעַד רַחְמָהּ.** כְּנֶגֶד רַחְמָהּ, וְכֵן כָּל לְשׁוֹן ׳בְּעַד׳:


**Her womb.** Lit., opposite her womb, and so is every expression of the word .¹²בְּעַד

footnotes:
¹² See Rashi, Bereishis 7:16.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:1)


**וְכֵן יַעֲשֶׂה.** אֶלְקָנָה:


**He would do this.** Elkonoh.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:7:2)


**שָׁנָה בְשָׁנָה.** נוֹתֵן לָהּ חֵלֶק מוּבְחָר, לְהַרְאוֹתָהּ שֶׁמְּחַבְּבָהּ. וְצָרָתָהּ, לְפִי חִבָּה שֶׁבַּעְלָהּ מַרְאֶה לָהּ, כֵּן תַּרְבֶּה גַּם הִיא לְהַכְעִיסָהּ:


**Year after year.** He would give her a choice portion to demonstrate to her that he loved her; and her rival, according to the affection which her husband demonstrated to her, would anger her more and more.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8:1)


**אָנֹכִי טוֹב לָךְ.** מְחַבְּבָךְ:


**Am I not better to you.** Don't I love you more dearly.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:8:2)


**מֵעֲשָׂרָה בָנִים.** שֶׁיָּלְדָה לִי פְּנִנָּה:


**Than ten sons.** That Penina has borne to me.¹³

footnotes:
¹³ Others interpret that Elkonoh did not mean literally ten sons and with reference to Penina, but rather he was expressing his love to Chana and happened to pick that number , as we find the same number in Vayikra 26:26.—Radak



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:1)


**אָכְלָה בְשִׁלֹה.** לֹא מַפִּיק ה״א, וְהָאַלֶ״ף חֲטַף קָמַ״ץ, וְהוּא כְּמוֹ: אַחֲרֵי אָכֹל בְּשִׁלֹה וְאַחֲרֵי שָׁתֹה. וְהַלָּשׁוֹן נוֹפֵל בֵּין לְזָכָר בֵּין לִנְקֵבָה, אַחֲרֵי אָכְלָה, לְשׁוֹן פָּעוּל, כְּמוֹ לְאָכְלָה, לְשׁוֹן לִפְעוֹל:


**Eating in Shilo.** The 'ה' is not dotted and the 'א' is voweled with a short *kametz* and its [meaning is] 'after eating in Shilo and after drinking,'¹⁴ and this expression is properly used both for masculine and feminine. After אָכְלָה is a gerund. It is constructed like ,¹⁵לְאָכְלָה an expression equivalent to an infinitive [i.e., לֶאֶכוֹל].

footnotes:
¹⁴ Eili therefore suspected that she was drunk because prayer usually takes place before eating or drinking—Da’as Sofrim.
¹⁵ Vayikra 25:6.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:2)


**וְעֵלִי הַכֹּהֵן יֹשֵׁב עַל הַכִּסֵּא.** אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם יָשַׁב עַל כִּסֵּא גָּדוֹל, שֶׁנִּתְמַנָּה שׁוֹפֵט (ובילקוט שמעוני: כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל) עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And Eili the kohen was sitting on the chair.** On that day he was seated on a large chair,¹⁶ for he was appointed Judge over Yisroel.

footnotes:
¹⁶ The defective spelling of ישֵׁב [instead of יוֹשֵׁב] indicates that he just recently sat down because he was a newly appointed judge. See Rashi Bereishis 19:1.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:9:3)


**עַל מְזוּזַת.** כְּמוֹ: אֵצֶל מְזוּזַת:


**Near the doorpost.** [Lit. on the doorpost] meaning near the doorpost.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:1)


**ה׳ צְבָאוֹת.** לָמָּה נִתְיַחֵד שֵׁם זֶה כָּאן, אָמְרָה לְפָנָיו: רִבּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, שְׁנֵי צְבָאוֹת בָּרָאתָ בְּעוֹלָמְךָ. הָעֶלְיוֹנִים, לֹא פָּרִים וְלֹא רָבִים וְלֹא מֵתִים. וְהַתַּחְתּוֹנִים, פָּרִים וְרָבִים וּמֵתִים. אִם אֲנִי מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים, אֶהְיֶה פָּרָה וְרָבָה וּמֵתָה, וְאִם אֲנִי מִן הָעֶלְיוֹנִים, לֹא אָמוּת. זוֹ מָצָאתִי בְּאַגָּדָה שֶׁל רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי (פסיקתא רבתי). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת בְּרָכוֹת (לא:) מַה שֶּׁדָּרְשׁוּ: עַד הֵנָּה לֹא הָיָה אָדָם שֶׁקְּרָאוֹ לְהַקָּבָּ״ה ׳צְבָאוֹת׳, אֶלָּא כָּךְ אָמְרָה לְפָנָיו: רִבּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, מִכָּל צְבָאוֹת שֶׁבָּרָאתָ בְּעוֹלָמְךָ קָשֶׁה בְּעֵינֶיךָ לִיתֵּן לִי בֵּן אֶחָד:


**Adonoy of Hosts.** Why was this Name designated here? She [Chana] said before Him, "Lord of the universe, You created two hosts in Your world. The heavenly beings neither multiply nor do they die; the earthly beings both multiply and die. If I am of the earthly beings, let me multiply and die, and if I am of the heavenly beings, let me not die." I found [this explanation] in the Aggadah of R. Eliezer the son of R. Yose Haglili. Our Rabbis in Maseches Berachos,¹⁷ expounded what they expounded: —Until then, there was no one person who called the Holy One, Blessed is He, [Master of] Hosts. She said thus before Him; "Lord of the universe, of all the hosts which You created in Your world, is it difficult for you to grant me one son?"


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:2)


**אִם רָאֹה תִרְאֶה.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ בוֹ בְּמַסֶּכֶת בְּרָכוֹת (שם) מַה שֶּׁדָּרְשׁוּ:


**If You look at.** In Maseches Berachos,¹⁸ our Sages expounded what they expounded.

footnotes:
¹⁸ Ibid.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:3)


**אֲמָתֶךָ.** הָאָמוּר שָׁלוֹש פְּעָמִים בַּמִּקְרָא, כְּנֶגֶד שְׁלֹשָׁה דְבָרִים שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה מְצֻוָּה עֲלֵיהֶם:


**Your maidservant.** [The word 'maidservant'] is stated three times in this verse, corresponding to the three mitzvos which a woman is commanded to observe.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ They are the laws of: 1. Menstruation .2 נִדָּה Separation of the dough .3 חַלָּה Kindling of the Shabbos lights [הַדְלָקַת הַנֵּר].


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:4)


**זֶרַע אֲנָשִׁים.** ״צַדִּיקִים, כְּמָה דְאַתְּ אָמַר (מלכים א ב:לב): ״בִּשְׁנֵי אֲנָשִׁים צַדִּיקִים:


**Male offspring.** I.e., righteous men, as it is written, "Two men [אֲנָשִׁים] who were righteous."²⁰

footnotes:
²⁰ I Melachim 2:32.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:5)


**אֲנָשִׁים.** ״חֲשׁוּבִים, כְּמָה דְאַתְּ אָמַר (דברים א:יג): ״אֲנָשִׁים חֲכָמִים וִידֻעִים:


**Males.** I.e., important men,²¹ as it is written, "wise… and known men ]אֲנָשִׁים[.²²

footnotes:
²¹ See Maseches Berachos 31b.
²² Devarim 1:13.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:6)


**וּנְתַתִּיו לַה׳.** שֶׁיְּהֵא רָאוּי לְתִתּוֹ לַה׳:


**Then I will give him to Adonoy.** I.e., that he be fit to be given to Adonoy.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:11:7)


**וּמוֹרָה.** ״תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּמָרוּת אֱנָשׁ לָא תְהֵא עֲלוֹהִי:


**And a razor.²³** Targum Yonoson renders, "and the fear [וּמַרְוַת] of man will not be upon him."

footnotes:
²³ See Maseches Nazir 66a where the very same difference of opinion is discussed. R. Nehorai renders מוֹרָה meaning a razor as Scripture states regarding Shimshon, in Shoftim 13:5, “the razor [מוֹרָה] must not be wielded on his head.” And R. Yose holds that [מוֹרָה] is a connotation referring to the fear of man.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:12:1)


**שֹׁמֵר אֶת פִּיהָ.** ״מְצַפֶּה מָתַי תַּפְסִיק, כָּךְ תִּרְגְּמוֹ יוֹנָתָן: ״וְעֵלִי אוֹרִיךְ לָהּ עַד דְּתִפְסוֹק:


**Watched her mouth.** He waited for her to stop, as Targum Yonoson renders, 'and Eili waited until she stopped.'


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:12:2)


**שֹׁמֵר.** לְשׁוֹן הַמְתָּנָה, כְּמוֹ: ״שָׁמַר אֶת הַדָּבָר״ (בראשית לז:יא); ״לֹא תִשְׁמֹר עַל חַטָּאתִי״ (איוב יד:טז):


**Watched.** An expression of waiting, as in, "[and his father] kept the matter in mind ]שָׁמַר["²⁴ i.e., waited, [and similarly] "do not wait [תִשְׁמוֹר] for my sin."²⁵

footnotes:
²⁴ Bereishis 37:11.
²⁵ Iyov 14:16.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:13:1)


**וַיַּחְשְׁבֶהָ עֵלִי לְשִׁכּוֹרָה.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ רְגִילִין לְהִתְפַּלֵּל בְּלַחַשׁ:


**Eili thought she was drunk.** Because they were not accustomed to praying silently.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:15:1)


**לֹא אֲדֹנִי.** לֹא אָדוֹן אַתָּה בְּדָבָר הַזֶּה, גִּלִּיתָ בְּעַצְמְךָ שֶׁאֵין רוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ שׁוֹרָה עָלֶיךָ שֶׁתֵּדַע שֶׁאֵינִי שִׁכּוֹרַת יַיִן:


**No, my lord.** You are not master in this matter. You have revealed that the Holy Spirit is not resting upon you, otherwise you would have known that I am not intoxicated from wine.²⁶

footnotes:
²⁶ Maseches Berachos 31b.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:1)


**אַל תִּתֵּן אֶת אֲמָתְךָ.** כְּלַפֵּי שֶׁאָמְרָה לוֹ דָּבָר קָשֶׁה, חָזְרָה וּפִיְּסָתוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא יִתְּנֶנָּה הֶפְקֵר וּבִזָּיוֹן לִפְנֵי צָרָתָהּ בַּת בְּלִיַּעַל:


**Do not deliver your maidservant.** Because she had spoken harshly to him, she tried to appease him, so that he deliver her not, unprotected and disgraced, to her rival, the wicked woman.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:2)


**כִּי מֵרֹב שִׂיחִי.** דִּבַּרְתִּי לְפָנֶיךָ קוֹשִׁי:


**For it is out of much grievance.** That I have spoken harshly to you.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:3)


**שִׂיחִי.** יֵשׁ מְקוֹמוֹת שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן אֲנִינוּת לֵב. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **מֵרֹב שִׂיחִי**; ״כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״אֲרֵי מִסְגֵי אַקְנָיוּתִי וְאַרְגָּזְוָתִי אוֹרֵיכִית בִּצְלוֹ עַד כְּעַן:


**Grievance.** In some instances, it [שִֹיחִי] is an expression of heartache. Another explanation of מֵרֹב שִֹיחִי, according to Targum, "because of much provocation and anger, I have prolonged my prayer until now."


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:4)


**וְכַעְסִי.** שֶׁצָּרָתִי מַכְעֶסֶת אוֹתִי:


**And anger.** My rival angers me.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:16:5)


**אַל תִּתֵּן.** לְשׁוֹן מְסִירָה, כְּמוֹ: ״אַל תִּתְּנֵנִי בְּנֶפֶשׁ צָרָי״ (תהלים כז:יב):


**Do not deliver.** An expression of giving over, as in, "'Do not deliver me to the will of my tormentors."²⁷

footnotes:
²⁷ Tehillim 27:12.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:17:1)


**אֶת שֵׁלָתֵךְ.** חָסֵר א׳, לִדְרוֹשׁ בּוֹ לְשׁוֹן בָּנִים, כְּמוֹ: ״וּבְשִׁלְיָתָהּ הַיּוֹצֵאת וְגוֹ׳״ (דברים כח:כז):


**Your request.** The א is missing, to infer [שֵׁלָתֵךְ] an expression of children,²⁸ as in, "and her young [וּבְשִׁלְיָתָהּ] who emerges from…."²⁹

footnotes:
²⁸ Others render שֵׁלָתֵך as being derived from שָׁלָל [=plunder], meaning that her soon to be born son will “take of the goods [שָׁלָל of the Torah,” i.e., he will gain much knowledge and understanding of the Torah.—Radak
²⁹ Devarim 28:57.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:17:2)


**יִתֵּן אֶת שֵׁלָתֵךְ.** בִּשְּׂרָהּ שֶׁנִּתְקַבְּלָה תְפִלָּתָהּ:


**Will grant the request.** He foretold to her that her prayer had been accepted.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18:1)


**תִּמְצָא שִׁפְחָתְךָ חֵן.** לְבַקֵּשׁ עָלֶיהָ רַחֲמִים:


**May your maidservant find favor.** To beg for compassion on her behalf.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:18:2)


**וּפָנֶיהָ לֹא הָיוּ לָהּ.** פָּנִים שֶׁל זַעַם:


**And no longer had the [sad] look on her face.** Meaning the face of anger.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:19:1)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ אֶל בֵּיתָם וַיֵּדַע אֶלְקָנָה.** מִכָּאן לְאַכְסְנַאי שֶׁאָסוּר בְּתַשְׁמִישׁ הַמִּטָּה:


**And came to their home… and Elkonoh knew.** From here [we learn] that a traveler is forbidden to have marital relations.³⁰

footnotes:
³⁰ Maseches Kesubos 65a.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:1)


**לִתְקֻפוֹת הַיָּמִים.** מִיעוּט תְּקוּפוֹת שְׁתַּיִם, מִיעוּט יָמִים שְׁנַיִם, לְשִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים וּשְׁנֵי יָמִים. מִכָּאן הַיּוֹלֶדֶת לְשִׁבְעָה, יוֹלֶדֶת לִמְקֻטָּעִים:


**And as the seasons and days passed.** The minimum of seasons [תְּקוּפוֹת] is two, and the minimum of days [יָמִים] is two. Hence, [she gave birth] after six months [=two seasons] and two days.³¹ From here [we learn] that a woman who gives birth in her seventh month of pregnancy, can give birth after entering part of the seventh month.

footnotes:
³¹ Maseches Rosh Hashanah 11a.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:20:2)


**שְׁמוּאֵל.** עַל שֵׁם אֵל וְעַל שֵׁם הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הוּא נִקְרָא, כִּי מִמֶּנּוּ שְׁאִלְתִּיו:


**Shmuel.** After the Name of God [א־ל], and in reference to the incident he was so called, for I requested [שָׁאַל] him of הַשֵׁם[=מא־ל].



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:1)


**אֶת זֶבַח הַיָּמִים.** (תרגום:) ״יָת דְּבַח מוֹעֲדַיָּא״:


**The festival offering.** [Targum renders] the sacrifices of the appointed seasons.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:21:2)


**וְאֶת נִדְרוֹ.** נְדָרִים שֶׁנָּדַר בֵּין רֶגֶל לְרֶגֶל הָיָה מַקְרִיב בָּרֶגֶל:


**And his vow.** The vows he made between festivals he would offer on the next festival.³²

footnotes:
³² Ibid., 6a.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:1)


**עַד יִגָּמֵל.** לְסוֹף עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁנַיִם (ספרים אחרים: עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה) חֳדָשִׁים, שֶׁכָּךְ זְמַן תִּינוֹק לִינַק:


**When [the boy] will be weaned.** At the end of twenty-two (other versions, twenty-four) months, for that is the period for a child to nurse.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:22:2)


**וְיָשַׁב שָׁם עַד עוֹלָם.** עוֹלָמָם שֶׁל לְוִיִּם חֲמִשִּׁים שָׁנָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וּמִבֶּן חֲמִשִּׁים שָׁנָה יָשׁוּב מִצְּבָא הָעֲבוֹדָה״ (במדבר ח:כה), וְכָךְ הָיוּ יָמָיו שֶׁל שְׁמוּאֵל, חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם, שֶׁהֲרֵי עֵלִי שָׁפַט אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה, וּבְיוֹם תְּפִלַּת חַנָּה נִתְמַנָּה שׁוֹפֵט, צֵא אוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה לְעִבּוּרוֹ שֶׁל שְׁמוּאֵל, נִשְׁתַּיְּרוּ שְׁלוֹשִׁים וָתֵשַׁע, וּשְׁמוּאֵל פִּרְנֵס אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל מִשֶּׁמֵּת עֵלִי שְׁלוֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי יוֹם שֶׁמֵּת עֵלִי גָּלָה הָאָרוֹן וְיָשַׁב בִּשְׂדֵה פְלִשְׁתִּים שִׁבְעָה חֳדָשִׁים (שמואל א ו:א), מִשָּׁם בָּא לְקִרְיַת יְעָרִים, עַד שֶׁהֶעֱלָהוּ דָוִד, מִשֶּׁמָּלַךְ שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים בְּחֶבְרוֹן עַל יְהוּדָה, וְהִמְלִיכוּהוּ כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל עֲלֵיהֶם, וּכְתִיב (שמואל א ז:ב): ״וַיְהִי מִיּוֹם שֶׁבֶת הָאָרוֹן בְּקִרְיַת יְעָרִים וַיִּרְבּוּ הַיָּמִים וַיִּהְיוּ עֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה״, צֵא מֵהֶן שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים שֶׁמָּלַךְ דָּוִד בְּחֶבְרוֹן, נִמְצָא מִשֶּׁגָּלָה הָאָרוֹן עַד שֶׁמֵּת שָׁאוּל שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְשִׁבְעָה חֳדָשִׁים, וּשְׁמוּאֵל מֵת לִפְנֵי שָׁאוּל אַרְבָּעָה חֳדָשִׁים:


**And shall remain there forever.** Forever in the Levitic sense,³³ i.e., fifty years as it is said, "from the age of fifty he shall retire from the work force."³⁴ Figuring as follows, we determine that Shmuel's lifetime was fifty-two years: Eili judged Yisroel for forty years.³⁵ On the day of Chana's prayer he was appointed judge.³⁶ By subtracting the year in which Chana conceived Shmuel, there remain thirty-nine years. Shmuel governed Yisroel from the time of Eili's death for thirteen years, as follows: On the day of Eili's death, the Ark was captured, and it remained in the land of the Philistines for seven months.³⁷ From there, it was brought to and remained in Kiryas Yearim until Dovid brought it up after he had reigned seven years in Chevron over Yehudah, and all of Yisroel had accepted him as their king. And it is written, "From the day the Ark came to rest in Kiryas Yearim, there ensued many days, and they became twenty years."³⁸ Subtracting from them seven years which Dovid reigned in Chevron discloses that from the time the Ark was captured until Shaul's death, were thirteen years and seven months; and Shmuel died four months before Shaul.

footnotes:
³³ It can also refer to the fifty year period of Yoveil.
³⁴ Bamidbar 8:25.
³⁵ I Shmuel 4:18.
³⁶ Above verse 9.
³⁷ Below 6:1.
³⁸ I Shmuel 7:2.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:23:1)


**אַךְ יָקֵם ה׳ אֶת דְּבָרוֹ.** אֶת שֶׁשָּׁאַלְתְּ מִמֶּנּוּ זֶרַע אֲנָשִׁים, וְעֵלִי בִּשְּׂרֵךְ בְּרוּחַ הַקּוֹדֶשׁ ״אֱלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יִתֵּן אֶת שֵׁלָתֵךְ״ – יָקֵם ה׳ אֶת דְּבָרוֹ, וְזֶהוּ לְפִי פְשׁוּטוֹ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: רַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה בְּשֵׁם רַבִּי שְׁמוּאֵל בַּר רַב יִצְחָק אָמַר: בְּכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם בַּת קוֹל יוֹצֵאת וּמְפוֹצֶצֶת בָּעוֹלָם וְאוֹמֶרֶת: עָתִיד צַדִּיק אֶחָד לַעֲמוֹד וּשְׁמוֹ שְׁמוּאֵל, וְכָל אִשָּׁה שֶׁהָיְתָה יוֹלֶדֶת בֵּן, הָיְתָה קוֹרְאָה שְׁמוֹ שְׁמוּאֵל, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהָיוּ רוֹאִים אֶת מַעֲשָׂיו, הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים אֵין זֶה שְׁמוּאֵל, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁנּוֹלַד זֶה וְרָאוּ מַעֲשָׂיו, אָמְרוּ דּוֹמֶה שֶׁזֶּה הוּא, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר: יָקֵם ה׳ אֶת דְּבָרוֹ, שֶׁיְּהֵא זֶה שְׁמוּאֵל הַצַּדִּיק:


**But may Adonoy fulfill His word.** Because you requested of Him male offspring,³⁹ and Eili foretold you through Divine inspiration, "the God of Yisroel will grant your request,"⁴⁰ may 'ה fulfill His word. This is the simple interpretation. In Midrash Aggadah, however, Rabbi Nechemyah said in the name of Rabbi Shmuel, the son of Rabbi Yitzchok: Every day a Divine voice would resound throughout the world and say, "A righteous man is destined to arise, by the name of Shmuel." Thereupon, every woman who bore a son, would name him Shmuel. Once they saw his deeds, they would say, "This is not [the] Shmuel." But when this one [Shmuel] was born and people saw his deeds, they said, "Apparently this one is the one [i.e., expected righteous man]." This is what he [Elkonoh] meant when he said, "May Adonoy fulfill His word," i.e., that this be the righteous Shmuel.

footnotes:
³⁹ Above, verse 11.
⁴⁰ Above, verse 17.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:1)


**וְאֵיפָה אַחַת קֶמַח.** שָׁמַעְתִּי בְּשֵׁם רַבֵּינוּ יִצְחָק הַלֵּוִי, אֵיפָה אַחַת קֶמַח, שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִין, לְהוֹצִיא מִמֶּנָּה שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים סֹלֶת הָרָאוּי לַפָּר הָאֶחָד, כְּדִתְנַן (מנחות עו:) ״לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע סְאִין״, עִשָּׂרוֹן סֹלֶת לִסְאָה חִטִּין:


**One *eiphah* of flour.** I heard in the name of Rabbi Yitzchok Halevi, that she brought one *eiphah* of flour, the equivalent of three *seahs,* from which to extract three tenths of an *eiphah* of fine flour, the required amount for one bull.⁴¹ As we learned in the Mishnah:⁴² The showbread was made of twenty-four tenths of an *eiphah* from twenty-four *seahs;* i.e., one tenth part of an *eiphah* of fine flour from each *seah* of wheat.

footnotes:
⁴¹ See Bamidbar 15:8-9.
⁴² Maseches Menachos 76b.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:2)


**וְנֵבֶל יָיִן.** לְנַסֵּךְ:


**And a flask of wine.** For a libation.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:24:3)


**וְהַנַּעַר נָעַר.** (תרגום:) ״וְרַבְיָא הֲוָה יָנִיק״:


**Though the child was still a lad.** The Targum renders, 'though the child was still young.'



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:25:1)


**וַיָּבִיאוּ אֶת הַנַּעַר אֶל עֵלִי.** לִרְאוֹת שֶׁנִּתְקַיְּמָה נְבוּאָתוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ מַה שֶּׁדָּרְשׁוּ, שֶׁהוֹרָה הֲלָכָה שֶׁאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ כֹּהֵן לִשְׁחִיטַת קָדָשִׁים, כִּדְאִיתָא בִּבְרָכוֹת (לא:):


**They brought the child to Eili.** To show that his prophecy was fulfilled. Our Sages expounded what they expounded, explaining that he [Shmuel] ruled on a point of law, that a kohein is not necessary for sacrificial slaughter, as it is stated in [Maseches] Berachos.⁴³

footnotes:
⁴³ 31b.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:26:1)


**בִּי אֲדֹנִי.** לָתֵת עֵינֶיךָ עָלָיו שֶׁיְּהֵא תַלְמִידְךָ. וּלְפִי מִדְרַשׁ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ: שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲנִישֵׁהוּ מִיתָה:


**Please my lord.** Take care of him, that he become your disciple. But according to the Midrash of our Sages, [she asked of Eili] not to punish him with death.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:27:1)


**אֶל הַנַּעַר הַזֶּה הִתְפַּלָּלְתִּי.** אַל תֹּאמַר: זֶה יָמוּת וְיִנָּתֵן לְךָ אַחֵר:


**This is the child that I prayed for.** Do not say, "this one shall die and another shall be given to you."⁴⁴

footnotes:
⁴⁴ Ibid.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:1)


**וְגַם אָנֹכִי הִשְׁאִלְתִּיהוּ לַה׳.** כְּאָדָם הַמַּשְׁאִיל כְּלִי לְרַבּוֹ, אוֹ מַשְׁאִילוֹ בְּנוֹ לְשַׁמְּשׁוֹ:


**And I have dedicated him to Adonoy.** Just as a person who lends a utensil to his master, or lends him his son to serve him.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:2)


**הוּא שָׁאוּל.** אנפרוצטי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּלוֹמַר אֵין אַתָּה רַשַּׁאי לְעָנְשׁוֹ, הַקָּבָּ״ה נַעֲשָׂה עָלָיו שׁוֹאֵל, כִּי הִשְׁאִלְתִּיו לוֹ, וְעָלָיו לְהַחֲזִירוֹ לִי:


**He is dedicated.** *Enpruntez* in Old French, i.e., you have no right to punish him, for the Holy one, Blessed is He, has become his borrower; because I have lent [Shmuel] to Him, He must return him to me.⁴⁵

footnotes:
⁴⁵ Another interpretation of “שָׁאוּל” is that it refers to Shaul. She stated by Divine inspiration, “as long as Shmuel lives, Shaul will live.” Shmuel was no longer alive at the time of Shaul’s death.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 1:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/1:28:3)


**וַיִּשְׁתַּחוּ שָׁם.** שְׁמוּאֵל, וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים אֶלְקָנָה:


**He then prostrated himself.** Shmuel. Others say, Elkonoh.



## Chapter 2


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:1:1)


**רָחַב פִּי עַל אוֹיְבַי.** עַל פְּנִינָה:


**My mouth is enlarged.** Against Penina.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:2:1)


**וְאֵין צוּר כֵּאלֹהֵינוּ.** אֵין צַיָּר כֵּאלֹהֵינוּ, הַצָּר צוּרָה בְּתוֹךְ צוּרָה:


**And there is no stronghold like our God.** There is no artist¹ [צַיָר] like our God Who fashions a form within a form.²

footnotes:
¹ Rashi disregards the usual rendering of צוֹר [=Rock], which represents the awesome powers of ‘ה Who is able to change the laws of nature. Seen Devarim 32:4.
² Maseches Berachos 10a.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:1)


**אַל תַּרְבּוּ תְדַבְּרוּ.** כָּל גַּסֵּי הָרוּחַ אֲשֶׁר שָׁעְתָם מַצְלַחַת, וּבִשְׁבִיל פְּנִינָה שֶׁהָיְתָה מִתְגָּאָה עָלֶיהָ הִיא מְדַבֶּרֶת, לְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ וּלְפִי דְרָשׁוֹ, כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ שֶׁתִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**Do not abound in speaking so very proudly.** All those who are haughty when they experience good fortune. About Penina who behaved haughtily toward her, was she speaking, according to the simple explanation. However, according to the *drash,* we follow Targum Yonoson's rendition [that she is referring to Nevuchadnetzar].


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:2)


**עָתָק.** דָּבָר חָזָק. דָּבָר אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן סָרָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּעְתֵּק מִשָּׁם הָהָרָה״ (בראשית יב:ח), יֵצֵא דְּבַר סָרָה מִפִּיכֶם:


**Harshness.** Strong speech. Others explain עָתָק as an expression of something which is removed [from the truth], as in "from there he moved [וַיַעְתֵּק] to the mountain."³ [The meaning here being "let not] falsehood come out of your mouth."

footnotes:
³ Bereishis 12:8.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:3)


**כִּי אֵל דֵּעוֹת ה׳.** יוֹדֵעַ מַה שֶׁבִּלְבַבְכֶם:


**For a God of knowledge is Adonoy.** He knows what is in your hearts.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:4)


**וְלוֹ נִתְכְּנוּ עֲלִילוֹת.** כָּל מַעֲשֵׂי הָאָדָם נִמְנִין לְפָנָיו:


**And by Him all actions are counted.** All people's deeds are counted before Him.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:3:5)


**נִתְכְּנוּ.** לְשׁוֹן מִנְיָן, כְּמוֹ ״וְתֹכֶן לְבֵנִים תִּתֵּנוּ״ (שמות ה:יח):


**Counted.** An expression of number, as in, "and the number [וְתֹכֶן] of bricks shall you give."⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Shemos 5:18.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:4:1)


**קֶשֶׁת גִּבּוֹרִים חַתִּים וְגוֹ׳.** כָּךְ אוּמָּנוּתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, מַתִּישׁ אֶת הַגִּבּוֹרִים וּמְחַזֵּק אֶת הַחַלָּשִׁים, מַשְׂבִּיעַ אֶת הָרְעֵבִים וּמַרְעִיב אֶת הַשְּׂבֵעִים:


**The bows of the mighty are broken…** This is the custom of the Holy One, Blessed is He. He weakens the mighty and strengthens the weak, He sates the hungry and starves the sated.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:1)


**שְׂבֵעִים בַּלֶּחֶם.** וְלֹא צְרִיכִים לִהְיוֹת נִשְׂכָּרִים לְשׁוּם מְלָאכָה, מַרְעִיבָם, וְנִשְׂכָּרוּ בַּלֶּחֶם פַּרְנָסָתָם:


**The sated have hired themselves out for bread.** [The sated] who do not need to hire themselves out for any work, He starves them and they must hire themselves out for bread,


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:2)


**וּרְעֵבִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ טוֹרְחִים וִיגֵעִים עַל מְזוֹנוֹת:


**And the hungry** Who would toil and weary themselves for food.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:3)


**חָדֵלוּ.** מִטָּרְחָם:


**Have ceased.** From their toil.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:5:4)


**עַד עֲקָרָה יָלְדָה שִׁבְעָה וְרַבַּת בָּנִים וְגוֹ׳.** וּבְעוֹד שֶׁהָעֲקָרָה יוֹלֶדֶת שִׁבְעָה בָנִים, רַבַּת בָּנִים אֻמְלָלָה וְקוֹבֶרֶת בָּנֶיהָ. חַנָּה יָלְדָה שִׁבְעָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שמואל א ב:כא) ״כִּי פָקַד ה׳ אֶת חַנָּה וַתַּהַר וַתֵּלֶד שְׁלֹשָׁה בָנִים וּשְׁתֵּי בָנוֹת״, וּכְשֶׁחַנָּה יוֹלֶדֶת אֶחָד, פְּנִינָה קוֹבֶרֶת שְׁנַיִם, וַעֲשָׂרָה בָנִים הָיוּ לָהּ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שמואל א א:ח): ״הֲלֹא אָנֹכִי טוֹב לָךְ מֵעֲשָׂרָה בָּנִים״, שֶׁל פְּנִינָה, כְּשֶׁיָּלְדָה חַנָּה אַרְבָּעָה, קָבְרָה פְּנִינָה שְׁמוֹנָה, וּכְשֶׁנִּתְעַבְּרָה וְיָלְדָה וָלָד חֲמִישִׁי, נִשְׁתַּטְּחָה פְּנִינָה לְרַגְלֶיהָ וּבִקְּשָׁה רַחֲמִים וְחָיוּ וְנִקְרְאוּ עַל שְׁמָהּ, אֵלּוּ דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה. רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר: בְּנֵי בָנִים הֲרֵי הֵן כְּבָנִים. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: שִׁבְעָה, בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא כְּמִנְיַן שְׁמוּאֵל:


**Until the barren has born seven and she with many children….** While the barren woman has born seven,⁵ she that had many children has been bereaved and buries her children. Chana bore seven as it is stated, "For Adonoy remembered Chana and she conceived and gave birth to three sons and two daughters." When Chana bore one, Penina buried two.⁶ Penina had ten children as it is stated, "Am I not better to you than [the] ten sons of Penina?"⁷ When Chana bore four, Penina buried eight. When she [Chana] conceived and bore a fifth child, Penina prostrated herself at her feet and begged for mercy and they lived and were therefore considered as hers. This is R. Nechemya's opinion. R. Yehudah however says that grandchildren are considered as children. Some are of the opinion that the numerical value of שִׁבְעָה(377) is equivalent to that of שְׁמוּאֵל(377).

footnotes:
⁵ Others interpret that the seven children mentioned are not literally seven and with reference to Peninah, but rather it is an expression that means “many children.” See above 1:8.—Radak
⁶ Below verse 21.
⁷ Above 1:8.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:9:1)


**חֲסִידָו.** חֲסִידוֹ כְּתִיב, חֲסִידָיו קְרִי, בֵּין יָחִיד בֵּין רַבִּים, וְכֵן ״יֵחַתּוּ מְרִיבָיו״ (פסוק י), מְרִיבוֹ, בֵּין יָחִיד בֵּין רַבִּים:


**His pious ones.** It is spelled חֲסִידוֹ "His pious *one"* but read חֲסִידָיו "His pious *ones,"* [meaning] whether a single one or many. Similarly "may His adversaries be broken." [The reading is מְרִיבָיו "His adversaries" but] the spelling is מְרִיבוֹ "His adversary," meaning whether a single adversary or many.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:1)


**עָלָיו בַשָּׁמַיִם יַרְעֵם.** עָלוּ כְּתִיב, אֲפִלּוּ עָלוּ בַשָּׁמַיִם, מַרְעִים עֲלֵיהֶם וּמוֹרִידָם:


**Let Him thunder against them from heaven.** The spelling is עָלוּ "they have ascended" meaning that even if they have ascended to the heavens, He thunders upon them and casts them down.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:10:2)


**יָדִין אַפְסֵי אָרֶץ.** שׁוֹפְטָן וּמְיַסְּרָן, יושטיצי״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**May Adonoy judge the ends of the earth.** He judges and punishes them. *Justisier* in O. F.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:11:1)


**הָיָה מְשָׁרֵת אֶת ה׳ אֶת פְּנֵי עֵלִי.** מִכָּאן לַמְּשַׁמֵּשׁ פְּנֵי תַּלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים, כִּמְשַׁמֵּשׁ פְּנֵי הַשְּׁכִינָה:


**Served Adonoy in the presence of Eili the kohein.** From here [our Sages conclude] that "whoever serves before a Torah scholar is considered as though he had served before the Divine Presence."⁸

footnotes:
⁸ Talmud Yerushalmi Eruvin 5:1. The reason being that a Torah scholar cleaves to ‘ה in his thoughts at all times so that even his physical activities are holy.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:13:1)


**וּמִשְׁפַּט הַכֹּהֲנִים.** הֵם קָבְעוּ לָהֶם חֹק זֶה, וְלָהֶם לֹא הָיָה לְנַחֲלָה כִּי אִם חָזֶה וָשׁוֹק שֶׁל שְׁלָמִים:


**The custom of the kohanim.** They established this law for themselves, since they rightfully inherited only the breast and the thigh of the peace offering.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:14:1)


**בַדּוּד.** סִיר:


**Pot.** Fire pot.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:14:2)


**בַּקַּלַּחַת.** יוֹרָה:


**Pan.** Cauldron.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:14:3)


**בַפָּרוּר.** מַחֲבַת:


**Bowl.** Pan.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:17:1)


**כִּי נִאֲצוּ.** לְשׁוֹן בִּזָּיוֹן:


**They had disgraced.** Meaning despised.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:18:1)


**נַעַר חָגוּר אֵפוֹד בָּד.** (תרגום:) ״כַּרְדּוּט דְּבוּץ״, וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן מְעִיל, שֶׁהֲרֵי תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן, (שמואל ב יג:יח): מְעִילֵי דְּ״כִּי כֵן תִּלְבַּשְׁנָה בְנוֹת הַמֶּלֶךְ הַבְּתוּלוֹת מְעִילִים״, (שמואל ב יג:יח): ״כַּרְדּוּטִין״:


**A lad girded with a linen robe.** [Targum renders] "כַּרְדּוּט of linen," meaning a robe, for Targum Yonoson renders מְעִילִים in the passage כִּי כֵן תִּלְבַּשְׁנָה הַבְּתוּלוֹת מְעִילִים "for such so מְעִילִים were worn by the maidens…",⁹ as כַּרְדוּטִין.

footnotes:
⁹ II Shmuel 13:18.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:19:1)


**וּמְעִיל קָטָן תַּעֲשֶׂה לוֹ אִמּוֹ.** מִשָּׁנָה לְשָׁנָה:


**His mother would make for him a small robe.** From year to year.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:20:1)


**וּבֵרַךְ עֵלִי.** לְשׁוֹן הוֹוֶה הוּא, מְבָרְכוֹ הָיָה בְּכָל שָׁנָה:


**Eili would then bless them.** This is the present tense, i.e., he would bless him every year.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:20:2)


**תַּחַת הַשְּׁאֵלָה אֲשֶׁר שָׁאַל.** לוֹ בֵּן, וְאָמַר לוֹ יָשִׂים ה׳ לְךָ זֶרַע וְגוֹ׳, יְהִי רָצוֹן שֶׁכָּל בָּנִים שֶׁיִּהְיוּ לְךָ, יִהְיוּ מִן הַצַּדֶּקֶת הַזּוֹ, וַהֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא מְסֹרָס:


**Because of the request that he had made.** For himself a son. [Eili] would say to him, "May השם grant you seed, etc., i.e., may it be the Divine Will that all the children that you will have, will be from this righteous woman. This is an inverted sentence.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:22:1)


**אֲשֶׁר יִשְׁכְּבוּן.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (יומא ט ב): מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁשָּׁהוּ אֶת קִנֵּיהֶן, וְהֵם הָיוּ מַמְתִּינוֹת עַד שֶׁתִּרְאֶןָ קִנֵּיהֶן קְרֵבִין, מַעֲלֶה עֲלֵיהֶם כְּאִלּוּ שְׁכָבוּם:


**That they would lie.** The explanation is according to the simple sense. Our Rabbis however said¹⁰ that since they delayed [the sacrifice of] their birds¹¹ and they would wait until they would see their birds being offered, Scripture charges them as though they had lain with them.¹²

footnotes:
¹⁰ In Maseches Yoma 9a-b.
¹¹ See Vayikra 15:29 and 22:6-8.
¹² See Maseches Shabbos 55b. Metzudas Dovid translates this phrase as “they would cause the women who assembled at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting to sleep there,” thereby not allowing them to return to their homes and husbands.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:24:1)


**אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי שׁוֹמֵעַ מַעֲבִירִים עַם ה׳.** שְׁמוּעָה שֶׁעַם ה׳ מַעֲבִירִים עֲלֵיכֶם, לְשׁוֹן ״וַיַּעֲבִירוּ קוֹל בַּמַּחֲנֶה״ (שמות לו:ו): מוֹצִיאִים עֲלֵיכֶם קוֹל שְׁמוּעָה לֹא טוֹבָה:


**That I hear being passed on by Adonoy's people.** The rumor which השם's people are spreading about you, an expression similar to, "and they proclaimed [וַיַעֲבִירוּ] throughout the camp;¹³ meaning, they let out a rumor about you which is not good.¹⁴

footnotes:
¹³ Shemos 36:6.
¹⁴ Although it was only a rumor, kohanim are held accountable even for an appearance of impropriety.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:25:1)


**וּפִלְלוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן מִשְׁפָּט, כְּמוֹ ״וְנָתַן בִּפְלִילִים״ (שמות כא:כב):


**Tries him.**וּפִלְלוֹ is an expression of judgment, as in, "and he shall pay by order of the judges ]פְלִילִים[."¹⁵

footnotes:
¹⁵ Shemos 21:22.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:25:2)


**אֱלֹהִים.** דַּיָּן:


**Judge.** The judge.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:25:3)


**כִּי חָפֵץ ה׳ לַהֲמִיתָם.** שֶׁכְּבָר נֶחְתַּם גְּזַר דִּין, אֲבָל קוֹדֶם שֶׁנֶּחְתַּם גְּזַר דִּין, נֶאֱמַר (יחזקאל יח:לב): ״כִּי לֹא אֶחְפֹּץ בְּמוֹת הַמֵּת״:


**For it was Adonoy's will to kill them.** For their verdict had already been sealed.¹⁶ Before the verdict is sealed however, Scripture states, "for I do not desire the death of one who should die."¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁶ See Maseches Rosh Hashanah 18b.
¹⁷ Yechezkeil 27:32.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:27:1)


**וַיָּבֹא אִישׁ אֱלֹהִים.** זֶה אֶלְקָנָה:


**The man of God came.** This was Elkonoh.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:27:2)


**הֲנִגְלֹה נִגְלֵיתִי אֶל בֵּית אָבִיךָ.** מִכָּאן שֶׁנִּתְנַבֵּא אַהֲרֹן בְּמִצְרַיִם, וּמַה הִיא הַנְּבוּאָה, הוּא שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (יחזקאל כ:ז): ״וָאוֹמַר אֲלֵיהֶם אִישׁ שִׁקּוּצֵי עֵינָיו הַשְׁלִיכוּ וּבְגִלּוּלֵי מִצְרַיִם אַל תִּטַּמָּאוּ״:


**Have I not repeatedly revealed Myself to your ancestor's family.** From here [our Sages concluded] that Aharon prophesied in Egypt. What was the prophecy? It is stated, "and I said to them, 'Every man, cast away the detestable idols of his eyes, do not defile yourselves with the idols of Egypt.'"¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ Ibid., 20:7.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:27:3)


**הֲנִגְלֹה נִגְלֵיתִי.** הֲיְדַעְתֶּם כִּי הַטּוֹבָה הַזֹּאת וְהַגְּדֻלָּה הַזֹּאת נָתַתִּי לְאַהֲרֹן:


**Have I not repeatėdly revealed Myself.** Did you know that this favor and greatness I gave to Aharon.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ Ralbag interprets this phrase as an affirmative statement, i.e., “I did indeed reveal myself to the…”



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:29:1)


**אֲשֶׁר צִוִּיתִי מָעוֹן.** אֲשֶׁר צִוִּיתִי בִּמְעוֹנִי:


**Which I commanded you [to bring in My] Sanctuary.** Which I commanded in My dwelling place.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:29:2)


**לְהַבְרִיאֲכֶם מֵרֵאשִׁית וְגוֹ׳.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא מְסֹרָס, וַתְּכַבֵּד אֶת בָּנֶיךָ מִמֶּנִּי לְעַמִּי, לְעֵינֵי עַמִּי כִּבַּדְתָּ אֶת בָּנֶיךָ מִמֶּנִּי, וּמַהוּ הַכָּבוֹד, לְהַבְרִיאֲכֶם מֵרֵאשִׁית כָּל מִנְחַת יִשְׂרָאֵל, קָדְמָה סְעוּדַתְכֶם לִסְעוּדָתִי, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (לעיל פסוק טו): ״גַּם בְּטֶרֶם יַקְטִירוּן אֶת הַחֵלֶב וְגוֹ׳״:


**To feed yourselves from the choice parts…** This is an inverted sentence [meaning], "you honor your sons more than Me ," ]לְעַמִּי[ before My people, i.e., in the eyes of My people, you honor your sons more than Me. And what is the honor? To feed yourselves from the choice parts of all the offerings of Yisroel. Your meal preceded My meal, as it is stated, "even before they would burn the fat…"²⁰

footnotes:
²⁰ Above verse 15.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:29:3)


**לְהַבְרִיאֲכֶם.** לְשׁוֹן סְעוּדָה, כְּמוֹ ״תָּבֹא נָא תָמָר אֲחוֹתִי וְתַבְרֵנִי לָחֶם״ (שמואל ב יג:ה):


**To feed yourselves.** [לְהַבְרִיאֲכֶם] is an expression of having a meal, as in, "Please let my sister Tamar come and serve [וְתַבְרֵנִי] me bread."²¹

footnotes:
²¹ II Shmuel 13:5.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:29:4)


**לְעַמִּי.** מוּסָב עַל </b>וַתְּכַבֵּד אֶת בָּנֶיךָ מִמֶּנִּי</b>, הֶרְאֵיתֶם לְעַמִּי שֶׁאַתֶּם נִכְבָּדִים מִמֶּנִּי, וּבַמֶּה הֶרְאֵיתֶם,</b>לְהַבְרִיאֲכֶם מֵרֵאשִׁית מִנְחָתִי׳</b>:


**My people.** This reverts to, "you honor your sons more than Me." You showed My people that you are more honored than I. And how have you shown this? By feeding yourselves from the choice parts of My offerings.²²

footnotes:
²² It may also be interpreted as referring to the part of the sacrifice that belonged to the people who brought the offering, that Eili’s sons took for themselves.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:30:1)


**אָמוֹר אָמַרְתִּי.** שְׁנֵי פְעָמִים פָּסַקְתִּי גְדֻלָּה לִבְנֵי אִיתָמָר, בִּבְנֵי גֵרְשׁוֹן וּבִבְנֵי מְרָרִי נֶאֱמַר (במדבר ד:כח), ״בְּיַד אִיתָמָר בֶּן אַהֲרֹן הַכֹּהֵן״, וְעֵלִי מִבְּנֵי אִיתָמָר הָיָה, זוֹ רָאִיתִי בְּמִדְרַשׁ שְׁמוּאֵל. וְלָשׁוֹן הָגוּן מִזֶּה שָׁמַעְתִּי: </b>אָמוֹר אָמַרְתִּי בֵּיתְךָ וּבֵית אָבִיךָ וְגוֹ׳</b>; מִתְּחִלָּה נָתַתִּי כְהוּנָה גְדוֹלָה לְאֶלְעָזָר הַכֹּהֵן, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במדבר כ:כו): ״וְהַפְשֵׁט אֶת אַהֲרֹן אֶת בְּגָדָיו וְגוֹ׳״, וּבִימֵי פִּלֶגֶשׁ שֶׁפָּקְרוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּרֹב הַמִּצְוֹת, וּמִי גָרַם לָהֶם, פִּנְחָס וְכַיּוֹצֵא בוֹ, שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם לְסַבֵּב מֵעִיר אֶל עִיר וּלְהוֹכִיחָם, נָטַלְתִּי הַכְּהוּנָה גְדוֹלָה מֵהֶם וּנְתַתִּיהָ לְךָ, שֶׁמִּבְּנֵי אִיתָמָר אַתָּה, וְאָמַרְתִּי יִתְהַלְּכוּ לְפָנַי עַד עוֹלָם, שֶׁכְּשֶׁפּוֹסְקִין לוֹ גְדֻלָּה לְאָדָם, פּוֹסְקִין לוֹ וּלְדוֹרוֹתָיו עַד עוֹלָם:


**I had indeed decreed.** Twice I assigned greatness to the sons of Isomor: Concerning the sons of Gershon and the sons of Merori, it is stated, "Under the direction of Isomor, the son of Aharon the kohein."²³ And Eili was one of the descendants of Isomor. This [explanation] I saw in Midrash Shmuel. I have, however, heard a more fitting explanation: "I had indeed decreed that your family and your father's family…" Originally I made Elozor the kohein to serve as kohein gadol, as it is stated, "remove Aharon's vestments, [and dress Elozor his son in them]."²⁴ However, at the time of the concubine,²⁵ Yisroel divested themselves of most mitzvos. And who was to blame [for this]? Pinchas and his associate kohanim, who should have gone around from city to city to reprove them. I therefore took the *kehunah gedolah* away from them, and gave it to you, for you are of Isomor's descendants, and I said, that they shall walk before Me forever; for when greatness is assigned to a person, it is assigned to him and to his generations forever.

footnotes:
²³ Bamidbar 4:33.
²⁴ Ibid., 20:26.
²⁵ See Shoftim Chapters 19-21.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:30:2)


**כִּי מְכַבְּדַי אֲכַבֵּד.** אֶת בְּנֵי פִנְחָס, שֶׁכִּבֵּד אוֹתִי בְּשִׁטִּים (במדבר כה:יא), וְכֵן הָיְתָה בִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה, כְּשֶׁנִּבְנָה בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, (מלכים א ב:כז): ״וַיְגָרֶשׁ שְׁלֹמֹה אֶת אֶבְיָתָר מִהְיוֹת כֹּהֵן לַה׳ כִּדְבַר ה׳ אֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר עַל בֵּית עֵלִי״, וְנַעֲשָׂה צָדוֹק כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל תַּחְתָּיו, שֶׁהָיָה מִבְּנֵי פִנְחָס, שֶׁכֵּן נִתְיַחֲסוּ בְדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים (א ו:לח):


**For I honor those who honor Me.** Pinchas' descendants who honored Me at Shittim.²⁶ And this happened in the days of Shlomo, that when the Temple was built, Shlomo dismissed Evyosor from being a *kohein [gadol]* to 'ה, fulfilling the word of 'ה which He had spoken concerning Eili's family.²⁷ And Tzadok became the *kohein gadol,* because he was of Pinchas' descendants; he is so listed in the genealogical records in Divrei Hayomim.²⁸

footnotes:
²⁶ Bamidbar 25:7-8.
²⁷ I Melochim 2:27.
²⁸ I Divrei Hayomim 6:35-38.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:30:3)


**וּבֹזַי יֵקָלּוּ.** מֵאֲלֵיהֶם, מִשֶּׁאֶסְתַּלֵּק מֵהֶם:


**And those who dishonor Me will be cursed.** By. themselves, when I will withdraw Myself from them.²⁹ would be removed in the time of Shlomo Hamelech.

footnotes:
²⁹ Referring to Eili’s sons from whom the kehunah gedolah



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:31:1)


**וְגָדַעְתִּי אֶת זְרֹעֲךָ.** הַחוֹזֶק שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָכֶם בְּבֵיתִי, שֶׁאַתֶּם אוֹמְרִים וְאִם לֹא לָקַחְתִּי בְחָזְקָה:


**I shall cut off your arm.** I.e., the power which you exert in My House, for you say, "or else I will take it by force."



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:32:1)


**וְהִבַּטְתָּ צַר מָעוֹן.** וְרָאִיתָ צָרָתְךָ בְּצִדְּךָ בְּתוֹךְ מְעוֹנִי, כְּאִשָּׁה הָרוֹאָה צָרָתָהּ עִמָּהּ בַּבַּיִת:


**And you will see a rival [in My] Sanctuary.** And you will see your rival at your side in My Sanctuary just as a woman who sees her rival with her in the house.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:32:2)


**בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר יֵיטִיב אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל.** כְּשֶׁיִּבָּנֶה בֵּית עוֹלָמִים בִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה, וְתְהֵא טוֹבָתָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל שְׁלֵמָה, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר שָׁם (מלכים א ח:נו): ״לֹא נָפַל דָּבָר אֶחָד מִכָּל דְּבָרוֹ הַטּוֹב״, (מלכים א ה:א) ״יְהוּדָה וְיִשְׂרָאֵל רַבִּים כַּחוֹל אֲשֶׁר עַל שְׂפַת הַיָּם וְגוֹ׳״. (מלכים א ה:ה) ״וַיֵּשֶׁב יְהוּדָה וְיִשְׂרָאֵל לָבֶטַח אִישׁ תַּחַת גַּפְנוֹ וְאִישׁ תַּחַת תְּאֵנָתוֹ כָּל יְמֵי שְׁלֹמֹה״:


**Throughout all the good that He will bring upon Yisroel.** When the Temple will be built in the days of Shlomo, and the goodness promised to Yisroel will be complete, as it is said there, "Not one word has gone unfulfilled from His entire gracious promise.³⁰ Yehudah and Yisroel were numerous, like the sand that is by the sea.³¹ And Yehudah and Yisroel dwelt in security, each man under his grapevine and under his fig tree… all the days of Shlomo."³²

footnotes:
³⁰ I Melochim 8:56.
³¹ Ibid. 4:20.
³² Ibid. 5:5.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:32:3)


**וְלֹא יִהְיֶה זָקֵן בְּבֵיתֶךָ.** מִדָּה כְּנֶגֶד מִדָּה, אַתֶּם אֲכַלְתֶּם קָדָשִׁים לִפְנֵי זְמַנָּן, בְּטֶרֶם יַקְטִירוּן אֶת הַחֵלֶב, אַף אַתֶּם תְּמוּתוּן בְּלֹא זְמַן:


**But there will be no elder in your family.** This is an appropriate punishment. You ate sacrifices before the [permitted] time, before they burnt the fat, so too, you will die before your time.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:33:1)


**וְלַאֲדִיב.** כְּמוֹ וּלְדַאִיב:


**And sadden.**וְלַאֲדִיב is the same as ³³וְלַדְאִיב [disillusioned].

footnotes:
³³ See Devorim 28:65.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:33:2)


**יָמוּתוּ אֲנָשִׁים.** בַּחוּרִים, אֵינוֹ דוֹמֶה אֶבְלוֹ שֶׁל בָּחוּר, לְאֶבְלוֹ שֶׁל תִּינוֹק:


**Will die as [young] men.** Young. There is no comparison between the mourning for a young man and the mourning for a child.³⁴

footnotes:
³⁴ Whose loss though tragic, is less keenly felt than the loss of a young man.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:34:1)


**וְזֶה לְךָ הָאוֹת.** שֶׁיִּתְקַיֵּם הַדָּבָר, בְּיוֹם אֶחָד יָמוּתוּ שְׁנֵי בָנֶיךָ, וְהִיא תִּהְיֶה לְךָ אוֹת שֶׁיִּתְקַיֵּם כָּל הַפּוּרְעָנוּת שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְךָ, וְהִבַּטְתָּ צָר מָעוֹן, וְכָל מַרְבִּית בֵּיתְךָ יָמוּתוּ אֲנָשִׁים:


**This is the sign to you.** That the prophecy will be fulfilled: In one day your two sons will die, and this will be the sign for you for the fulfillment³⁵ of all the retribution that was said to you, "and you will see a rival in My Sanctuary," "and all those raised in your house will die as [young] men."³⁶

footnotes:
³⁵ He needed a sign in the near future to authenticate the prophecy that all the punishments described will come true.
³⁶ Above verses 32-33.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:35:1)


**כֹּהֵן נֶאֱמָן.** הוּא צָדוֹק:


**A faithful kohein.** Tzadok.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:36:1)


**לַאֲגוֹרַת כָּסֶף.** בִּשְׁבִיל לְהִשְׂתַּכֵּר מָעָה כֶּסֶף:


**For a silver coin.** In order to earn a מָעָה silver coin.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:36:2)


**לַאֲגוֹרַת כָּסֶף.** הוּא מִמִּשְׁקָלוֹת: ״עֶשְׂרִים גֵּרָה״ (שמואל ל:יג):


**Silver coin.** with a weight of twenty *gerah.*³⁷

footnotes:
³⁷ Shemos 30:13.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 2:36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/2:36:3)


**סְפָחֵנִי.** אָסְפֵנִי:


**Let me be included.** Let me to join.



## Chapter 3


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:1:1)


**הָיָה יָקָר.** הָיָה מָנוּעַ, וְכֵן ״יְקַר רוּחַ אִישׁ תְּבוּנָה״ (משלי יז:כז), מָנוּעַ דְּבָרִים, אָדָם מֵבִין:


**Was rare.** Was withheld,¹ as in, "and a man of understanding speaks sparingly ]יֲקר[",² meaning that who speaks sparingly is an understanding man.

footnotes:
¹ It was therefore considered precious and dear [=יָקָר].
² Mishlei 17:27.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:1:2)


**אֵין חָזוֹן נִפְרָץ.** (תרגום:) לֵית נְבוּאָה גַּלְיָא. נִפְרָץ - לָשׁוֹן ״וּפָרַצְתָּ יָמָּה וָקֵדְמָה״ (בראשית כח:יד):


**Prophetic vision was not widespread.** Prophecy was not revealed.³נִפְרָץ is an expression similar to, "and you shall spread [וּפָרַצְתָּ] westward and eastward.⁴

footnotes:
³ Not only were prophetic visions rare in those days, but even when they occurred they were not clear and concise, but rather cryptic and vague. Also, the prophecy that was received was of a private nature and not required by the people, so there was no need to reveal those visions. The prophets therefore kept their experiences to themselves and prophetic vision was rare and not widespread.
⁴ Bereishis 28:14.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:3:1)


**וּשְׁמוּאֵל שֹׁכֵב.** בִּמְקוֹמוֹ בְּעֶזְרַת הַלְוִיִּם הַשּׁוֹמְרִים בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּשְׁמוּאֵל שָׁכִיב בְּעֶזְרַת לֵוָאֵי״:


**And Shmuel was lying.** In his place, in the court of the Levites who watch in the Temple. According to Targum Yonoson: "And Shmuel was lying in the court of the Levites."


###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:3:2)


**בְּהֵיכַל ה׳** אֲשֶׁר שָׁם אֲרוֹן הָאֱלֹהִים:


**In the Temple of Adonoy.** Where the Ark of God was.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:4:1)


**וַיִּקְרָא ה׳** וְקוֹל יָצָא מֵהֵיכַל ה׳ מִבֵּית קָדְשֵׁי הַקֳּדָשִׁים, שֶׁקָּרָא שְׁמוּאֵל, סוֹף הַמִּקְרָא הָעֶלְיוֹן מְחֻבָּר לְרֹאשׁ הַמִּקְרָא הַתַּחְתּוֹן, זֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר הַכָּתוּב ״יַרְעֵם אֵל בְּקוֹלוֹ נִפְלָאוֹת״ (איוב לז:ה), עֵלִי הָיָה כֹּהֵן וְשׁוֹמֵר מִבִּפְנִים, וּשְׁמוּאֵל הָיָה לֵוִי וְשׁוֹכֵב מִבַּחוּץ, וְקָפַץ הַקּוֹל דֶּרֶךְ עֵלִי לִשְׁמוּאֵל, בְּמַסֶּכֶת תָּמִיד:


**Adonoy called.** And a voice emanated from the Temple of God, from the Holy of Holies, which called, "Shmuel!" The end of the above verse⁵ is connected to the verse that follows. This is what is inferred by the verse, "the Lord thunders wonderously with His voice."⁶ Eili was a kohein, and was watching from within, while Shmuel was a Levite and lying without. Yet, the voice [of 'ה] jumped over Eili to Shmuel.

footnotes:
⁵ As is indicated by the אֶתְנַחְתָּא under the word שֹׁכֵכ to separate it from ‘בְּהֵיכַל ה.
⁶ Iyov 37:5.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:7:1)


**טֶרֶם יָדַע אֶת ה׳.** עֲדַיִן לֹא הָיָה מַכִּיר עִנְיַן קוֹל נְבוּאָה, כָּל ׳טֶרֶם׳ שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא, לְשׁוֹן ׳עֲדַיִן לֹא׳, כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״עַד לָא״, וְאֵינוֹ לְשׁוֹן ׳לִפְנֵי׳, וְזֶה יוֹכִיחַ, וְעוֹד, ״כִּי טֶרֶם תִּירְאוּן״ (שמות ט:ל), מְפֹרָשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ, וְכֵן ״וְכָל שִׂיחַ הַשָּׂדֶה טֶרֶם יִהְיֶה בָאָרֶץ״ (בראשית ב:ה):


**[Shmuel] had not yet known Adonoy.** He had not yet recognized the nature of the prophetic voice.⁷ Every time the word 'טֶרֶם' appears in Scripture, it means 'not yet,' as rendered by the Targum 'עַד לָא' [not yet]. It does not mean 'before.' This verse proves it. And another [proof of this] is the verse כִּי טֶרֶם תִּירְאוּן which is rendered as "that you do not yet fear,"⁸ is a more explicit verse proving this. Also, וְכֹל שִֹיחַ הַשָֹּדֶה טֶרֶם יִהְיֶה בָאָרֶץ ["all plants of the field were not yet on the earth"].⁹

footnotes:
⁷ He was thus unfamiliar with the ways ‘ה calls man, and he therefore thought that the voice was Eili’s.
⁸ Shemos 9:30.
⁹ Bereishis 2:5.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:10:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר שְׁמוּאֵל דַּבֵּר.** וְלֹא הִזְכִּיר שֵׁם מָקוֹם כְּמוֹ שֶׁצִּוָּהוּ עֵלִי, אָמַר שֶׁמָּא קוֹל אַחֵר הוּא:


**And Shmuel said, "Speak."** He did not mention the Name of the Deity as Eili had commanded him, because he said [to himself], 'Perhaps it is another voice.'¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ See Maseches Shabbos 113b.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:11:1)


**עֹשֶׂה דָבָר.** הִיא לְקִיחַת הָאָרוֹן בְּיַד פְּלִשְׁתִּים:


**To do something.** This refers to the capture of the Ark by the Philistines.¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ Also, this may be referring to the destruction of the Mishkon in Shiloh which happened after the Ark was captured.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:11:2)


**תְּצִלֶּינָה.** לְשׁוֹן צִלְצָלִים, טנטיני״ר בלע״ז:


**Will ring.** An expression similar to [צִלְצָלִים], cymbals, *tintiner,* in O. F.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:12:1)


**הָחֵל וְכַלֵּה.** אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם תַּתְחִיל גְּזֵרָתִי עַל בֵּית עֵלִי, שֶׁיָּמוּתוּ חָפְנִי וּפִינְחָס, וּמִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ אֲכַלֶּה גְּזֵרָתִי וְאַשְׁלִימָה עֲלֵיהֶם מִדּוֹר אֶל דּוֹר, לַהֲמִיתָם אֲנָשִׁים בְּנֵי שְׁמוֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה:


**I will begin to destroy.** On that day, My decree upon Eili's family will begin, for Chofni and Pinchas shall die, and from then on, I shall finish my decree and complete it upon them from generation to generation, and cause them to die as young men at the age of eighteen years.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:12:2)


**הָחֵל וְכַלֵּה.** אקומיינצ״ט אאישפליימנ״ט בלע״ז:


**I will begin to destroy.** *Commencant e espleiant,* in O. F.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:13:1)


**כִּי מְקַלְלִים לָהֶם בָּנָיו.** מְקַלְלִים ׳לִי׳ הָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר, אֶלָּא שֶׁכִּנָּה הַכָּתוּב:


**That his sons were blaspheming themselves.** It should properly state, 'blaspheming Me' but Scripture euphemized.¹²

footnotes:
¹² See Mechilta Beshalach 15:7 for a listing of other places where Scripture euphemized out of reverence to ‘ה. Ralbag, however, interprets this verse that “they [Bnei Yisroel] cursed them [the sons of Eili].”


###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:13:2)


**מְקַלְלִים.** מְקִילִים, וְכֵן כָּל קְלָלָה, לְשׁוֹן קַלּוּת וּבִזָּיוֹן הוּא:


**Blaspheming.** Meaning, acting irreverantly, and similarly, every curse [קְלָלָה] is an expression of irreverence and disgrace.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:13:3)


**וְלֹא כִהָה.** לֹא הִכְהָה פְּנֵיהֶם לְהַעֲבִירָם מִגְּדֻלָּתָן:


**He did not censure.** I.e., he did not darken [כהה] their faces by removing them from their high positions.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:17:1)


**כֹּה יַעֲשֶׂה לְּךָ.** כְּאוֹתָהּ קְלָלָה עַצְמָהּ, שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה לְךָ בָּנִים הֲגוּנִים, וְכֵן עָלְתָה בּוֹ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִגִּיד לוֹ וְלֹא כִּחֵד, מִכָּאן אָמְרוּ (מכות יא א): ״קִלְלַת חָכָם אֲפִלּוּ עַל תְּנַאי הִיא בָּאָה״:


**Such shall [God] do to you.** Like that very curse, that you shall not have worthy children. And that curse actually befell him even though he did tell him. **And did not withhold.** From here [the Sages concluded]: A curse of a wise man, even though pronounced on condition, will be realized.¹³

footnotes:
¹³ Maseches Makos 11a.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:18:2)


**ה׳ הוּא.** אָדוֹן הוּא, וְהַכֹּל שֶׁלּוֹ:


**He is Adonoy.** He is the Master and everything is His.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Samuel 3:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/3:21:1)


**כִּי נִגְלָה ה׳ אֶל שְׁמוּאֵל.** לְפִי שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה וּדְבַר ה׳ הָיָה יָקָר בַּיָּמִים הָהֵם, אָמַר כָּאן כִּי מֵעַתָּה בִּשְׁבִיל שְׁמוּאֵל, נִגְלָה וְשָׁנָה וְהִרְגִּיל לְהִגָּלוֹת:


**For Adonoy revealed Himself to Shmuel.** Because Scripture stated above, "The word of Adonoy was rare in those days,"¹⁴ it states here that from now on, for Shmuel's sake, He revealed Himself repeatedly.¹⁵

footnotes:
¹⁴ Above 3:1.
¹⁵ Not only to Shmuel, but others also began to prophesy.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:1:1)


**וַיְהִי דְבַר שְׁמוּאֵל לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל.** נִהְיָה הַדָּבָר שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לִשְׁמוּאֵל, וּבָא לְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְהָאֵיךְ הָיָה הַפּוּרְעָנוּת, וַיֵּצֵא יִשְׂרָאֵל לִקְרַאת פְּלִשְׁתִּים לַמִּלְחָמָה:


**The word of Shmuel went out to all of Yisroel.** The word which was said to Shmuel, was fulfilled, and came to all Yisroel. How did this misfortune befall them? "Yisroel went out to war against the Philistines."¹

footnotes:
¹ When Bnei Yisroel deserved punishment, ‘ה sometimes commanded them to go out and battle the Philistines even though it would end in their defeat.—Radak


###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:1:2)


**הָאֶבֶן הָעֵזֶר.** עֲדַיִן לֹא הָיָה כֵּן שְׁמָהּ, כִּי בִּימֵי שְׁמוּאֵל נִקְרֵאת כֵּן (שמואל א ז:יב), וְכָאן קָרָא עַל שֵׁם סוֹפָהּ:


**Even Haezer** [Lit. the rock of help]. This was not yet its name,² for it was first called this name [later] in Shmuel's time, but here Scripture calls it by its future name.

footnotes:
² Obviously, because they were soundly defeated now.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:2:1)


**וַתִּטֹּשׁ הַמִּלְחָמָה.** וַתִּתְפַּשֵּׁט הַמִּלְחָמָה, כְּמוֹ ״וְהִנָּם נְטוּשִׁים עַל פְּנֵי הָאָרֶץ״ (שמואל א ל:טז), וְכֵן ״וַיִּנָּטְשׁוּ בַּלֶּחִי״ (שופטים טו:ט):


**And the war spread.** And the war spread. Similar to, "and behold they were spread out [נְטוּשִׁים] across the face of the entire land"³ and to "and they were spread out [וַיִנָּטְשׁוּ] at Lehi."⁴

footnotes:
³ Below 30:16.
⁴ Shoftim 15:9.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:8:1)


**בְּכָל מַכָּה בַמִּדְבָּר.** עַל יַם סוּף וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וּלְעַמֵּיהּ עֲבַד פְּרִישָׁן בְּמַדְבְּרָא״. וּבְסִפְרֵי שָׁנִינוּ: שֶׁהַפָּרָשָׁה הַזּוֹ עֵרוּב דְּבָרִים, מִי שֶׁאָמַר זֶה לֹא אָמַר זֶה, כְּשֵׁרִין שֶׁבָּהֶם אָמְרוּ: מִי יַצִּילֵנוּ מִיַּד הָאֱלֹהִים הָאַדִּירִים הָאֵלֶּה, וְהָרְשָׁעִים אָמְרוּ: אֵלֶּה הֵם הָאֱלֹהִים הַמַּכִּים אֶת מִצְרַיִם בְּכָל מַכָּה, כָּל מַכּוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ, עַל הַמִּצְרִים הֱבִיאָם, אֵין לוֹ עוֹד מַכָּה מֵעַתָּה:


**With every sort of plague in the wilderness.** By the Sea of Reeds. Targum Yonoson however, renders, 'and for His people He performed wonders in the wilderness.' In Sifrei we learn that this passage is a combination of statements, whoever made one statement did not make the other. The worthy among them said, "Who will save us from the hand of this mighty God?" while the wicked retorted, "This is the God Who smote the Egyptians with every plague. All the plagues which He had, He brought upon the Egyptians. He has no more plagues [to inflict]."



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:12:1)


**אִישׁ בִּנְיָמִן.** זֶה הָיָה שָׁאוּל, שֶׁחָטַף אֶת הַלּוּחוֹת מִיַּד גָּלְיָת וּבָרַח לוֹ:


**A Binyaminite man.** This was Shaul,⁵ who grabbed the Tablets from the hands of Golyas and fled.

footnotes:
⁵ Perhaps the reason for Scripture not revealing that it was Shaul, was to protect the honor of his kingship being that he was responsible in part for Eili’s death.—Da’as Sofrim



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:13:1)


**יַד דֶּרֶךְ מְצַפֶּה.** אֵצֶל יַד הַדֶּרֶךְ הָיָה מְצַפֶּה:


**By the road looking out.** By the side of the road, he was anxiously waiting.⁶

footnotes:
⁶ He wanted to be situated in a place where a messenger would have to pass him first, and thus give him the news.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:18:1)


**מַפְרַקְתּוֹ.** אַפַּרְקוּתֵיהּ, עֶצֶם הַצַּוָּאר:


**His neck.** [Targum renders] אַפַּרְקוּתֵיה, the bone of the neck.⁷

footnotes:
⁷ מַפְרֶקֶת is derived from פֶּרֶק [=link], meaning vertebra of which the bones of the neck and spine are made.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:19:1)


**לָלַת.** עַל כָּרְחוֹ ׳לָלֶדֶת׳ פֵּרוּשׁוֹ, וְאֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ בְּחֵלֶק ׳יְלָלָה׳, לְפִי שֶׁחֶבְלֵי לֵידָתָהּ בִּילָלָה בָּאוּ לָהּ, וִיסוֹד יְלָלָה, אֵין אוֹת בִּיסוֹדָהּ אֶלָּא לָמֶ״ד לְבַדָּהּ:


**To give birth.** Obviously [this word] means 'to give birth.' There is no similar form [in Scriptures]. However, Menachem⁸ placed this verb in the section dealing with the word יְלָלָה 'wailing,' because her birth pangs came upon her with wailing. The root of 'יְלָלָה' is the 'ל' alone.

footnotes:
⁸ Menachem was an authority on grammar and is often quoted by Rashi.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:19:2)


**נֶהֶפְכוּ.** נִשְׁתַּנּוּ מִכְּדַרְכָּן, לְכָךְ מֵתָה:


**Came suddenly.** Differed from what normally occurs, therefore she died.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:19:3)


**צִרֶיהָ.** צִירֵי דַּלְתֵי בִּטְנָהּ, קרדוני״ל בלע״ז:


**Her labor pains.** [צִרֶיהָ=hinges], i.e., 'the hinges of the doors of her womb';⁹*chardonel* in O. F.

footnotes:
⁹ See Bechoros 45a.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21:1)


**אִי כָבוֹד.** אֵין כָּבוֹד, וְכֵן ״יְמַלֵּט אִי נָקִי״ (איוב כב:ל), אֵין נָקִי:


**I chavod.** There is no glory; similar to, "He will save "¹⁰אִי נָקִי one who is not innocent, [meaning the same as] אֵין נָקִי.

footnotes:
¹⁰ Iyov 22:30.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21:2)


**אֶל הִלָּקַח.** כְּמוֹ עַל הִלָּקַח, וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לְזוּזוֹ מִלְּשׁוֹן אֶל, לְמִי שֶׁיּוֹדֵעַ לְהָבִין דְּבָרוֹ:


**Because of the capture of.** Meaning the same as 'to the capture of', but it is unnecessary to remove the expression of 'to' for one who understands its meaning.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21:3)


**אֶל הִלָּקַח.** איטיויר״ש לאישטריפו״ש בלע״ז:


**Because of the capture of.** *Envers etweirs l'estre pris* in O. F.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 4:21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/4:21:4)


**וְאֶל חָמִיהָ.** וְאֶל הָרָעָה הַזֹּאת, שֶׁמֵּת חָמִיהָ וְאִישָׁהּ:


**And because of her father-in-law.** I.e., because of this misfortune that her father-in-law and husband had perished.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on I Samuel 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:2:1)


**אֵצֶל דָּגוֹן.** דְּמוּת עָשׂוּי כִּדְמוּת דָּג:


**Beside Dagon.** The idol was made in the form of a fish.¹

footnotes:
¹ From the word דָג [=fish]. Some say that from the waist up it had a human form with hands, and from the waist down it looked like a fish.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Samuel 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:4:1)


**הַמִּפְתָּן.** אַסְקוּפָּה:


**The threshold.** The equivalent of אַסְקוּפָה [=threshold].



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Samuel 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:6:1)


**וַיְשִׁמֵּם.** לְשׁוֹן שְׁמָמָה:


**And He devastated them.** An expression [שְׁמָמָה] meaning desolation.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:6:2)


**בַּטְּחוֹרִים.** חַלְחֹלֶת, כַּרְכְּשָׁא, מַכַּת הַנֶּקֶב, עַכְבָּרִים נִכְנָסִין בְּנִקְבֵיהֶם וְשׁוֹמְטִין בְּנֵי מְעֵיהֶם, וְיוֹצְאִין:


**With hemorrhoids.** The mesentery of the large intestine, a plague of the rectum. Mice would enter their recta, disembowel them, and crawl out.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Samuel 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/5:9:1)
