**מוּל גָּבַע.** שֶׁמִּמֶּנָּה יָצָא יְהוֹנָתָן:


**Toward Geva.** From where Yonoson emerged.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:9:1)


**דֹּמּוּ.** הַמְתִּינוּ, כְּמוֹ ״שֶׁמֶשׁ בְּגִבְעוֹן דּוֹם״ (יהושע י:יב); וְכֵן ״דּוֹם לַה׳״ (תהלים לז:ז), אִם כֹּה יֹאמְרוּ: דּוֹמוּ וְנַעֲלֶה אֲלֵיכֶם, שְׁעָתָם מַצְלַחַת וְלִבָּם רָם עֲלֵיהֶם:


**Stop.** Wait, as in, "Sun, wait [דּוֹם] in Givon,"² and as in, "Wait for ."³השם [The meaning here being] if they say, "Wait, and we will go up to you," they are presently successful and they are proud.

footnotes:
² Yehoshua 10:12.
³ Tehillim 37:7.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:10:1)


**וְאִם כֹּה יֹאמְרוּ עֲלוּ עָלֵינוּ.** פַּחַד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּלִבָּם, וִירֵאִים לָזוּז מִמְּקוֹמָם:


**But if they say [to us], 'Come up to us.'** A fear inspired by the Holy One, Blessed is He, is in their heart and they are afraid to move from their place.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Although there is a prohibition against resorting to omens in Vayikra 19:26, Tosfos in Maseches Chulin 95b explains that Yonoson himself did not believe in the sign, he only made it to encourage his weapon bearer. See Radak.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:12:1)


**וַיַּעֲנוּ.** לְשׁוֹן הֲרָמַת קוֹל:


**[They] called out.** An expression of raising [one's] voice.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:13:1)


**עַל יָדָיו וְעַל רַגְלָיו.** כְּלוֹמַר בְּכָל כֹּחַ וּמְרוּצָה:


**On his hands and feet.** I.e., with all his might and running.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ Rashi explains עַל יָדָיו וֽעַל רַגְלָיו as an idiomatic expression; but Radak explains it literally, that they had to use their hands and feet to climb because it was a very steep and slippery crag.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:14:1)


**כַּבֲחצִי מַעֲנָה צֶמֶד שָׂדֶה.** בְּתוֹךְ שִׁיעוּר מִדַּת קַרְקַע, מְלֹא חֲצִי מַעֲנָה שֶׁל מַחֲרֵשַׁת צֶמֶד בָּקָר הַחוֹרֶשֶׁת בַּשָּׂדֶה:


**Half a furrow, of a pair [of oxen plowing] in the field.** Within the area of a land measure [equal to] half the amount of a furrow of the plowing of a pair of oxen plowing a field.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:14:2)


**מַעֲנָה.** הוּא תֶּלֶם הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה, שֶׁקּוֹרִין ריי״א בְּלַעַ״ז, וּגְבוּרָה גְדוֹלָה הִיא זוֹ, שֶׁהָיוּ קְרוֹבִים זֶה לָזֶה, וּנְכוֹנִים לַעֲזוֹר זֶה אֶת זֶה:


**Furrow.** That is the ridge which is called *'reie'* in O.F. This was a very mighty deed, because they were near each other and ready to help each other.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:16:1)


**הַצֹּפִים לְשָׁאוּל.** הַצֹּפִים אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ לְשָׁאוּל בְּגִבְעַת בִּנְיָמִן, צוֹפִים אישקאגט״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Shaul's sentries.** The sentries that Shaul had in Givas Binyomin.⁶צוֹפִים is *badetes* in O.F.

footnotes:
⁶ The sentries appointed by Shaul to keep watch on the enemy made the observation.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:16:2)


**וְהִנֵּה הֶהָמוֹן.** שֶׁל פְּלִשְׁתִּים, נָמוֹג מִמְּקוֹמוֹ וַיֵּלֶךְ, וְקָרַב הֲלוֹם לְצַד יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**Behold the multitude.** Of the Philistines melted away from its position and kept coming nearer to הֲלֹם i.e., Yisroel's side.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:16:3)


**נָמוֹג.** כְּמוֹ ״נָע וָנָד״ (בראשית ד:יב):


**Melting away.** [It means the same as], "wandering and moving."⁷

footnotes:
⁷ Bereishis 4:12.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:17:1)


**פִּקְדוּ.** לְשׁוֹן פְּקִידָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַתִּפְקְדֶנּוּ לִבְקָרִים״ (איוב ז:יח):


**Inspect.** An expression of inspection, as in, "that You inspect him every morning."⁸

footnotes:
⁸ Iyov 7:18.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:18:1)


**הַגִּישָׁה אֲרוֹן הָאֱלֹהִים.** אוּרִים וְתֻמִּים:


**Bring near the Ark of God.** I.e., the *Urim* and *Tumim.*


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:18:2)


**כִּי הָיָה אֲרוֹן הָאֱלֹהִים בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא.** הַמִּקְרָא חָסֵר תֵּיבָה אַחַת, כִּי הָיָה אֲרוֹן הָאֱלֹהִים שָׁם בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא:


**For the Ark of God was there on that day.** This verse is missing one word; [what it means is,] "because the Ark of God was there [שָׁם] on that day."



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:21:1)


**וְהָעִבְרִים הָיוּ לַפְּלִשְׁתִּים.** בְּעֶזְרָתָם, מֵחֲמַת יִרְאָה מִתְּמוֹל שִׁלְשׁוֹם, וְהַיּוֹם נֶהֶפְכוּ גַּם הֵמָּה עִם חַבְרֵיהֶם, לִהְיוֹת בְּעֶזְרַת אֲחֵיהֶם:


**And the Hebrews who were with the Philistines.** To help them, out of fear, in earlier times;⁹ but today they too turned [against the Philistines and joined] their fellows to be at their brethren's assistance.

footnotes:
⁹ The Bnei Yisroel who had been living in Philistine territory had been forcibly drafted into the Philistine army to fight against their brethren.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:24:1)


**נִגַּשׂ בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא.** הָיוּ אָצִים וּנְגוּשִׁים לְהִלָּחֵם בַּפְּלִשְׁתִּים:


**Were hard pressed on that day.** They were being rushed and pressed¹⁰ to wage war against the Philistines.

footnotes:
¹⁰ As in Shemos 5:13; Metzudos renders “approached.”


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:24:2)


**וַיּוֹאֶל.** לְשׁוֹן אָלָה:


**[Shaul] had imposed an oath.** An expression of oath.¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ Shaul did not want the Bnei Yisroel to interrupt their pursuit of the Philistines; he therefore bound the Bnei Yisroel with an oath that no one should eat any food until the evening.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:24:3)


**אֲשֶׁר יֹאכַל לֶחֶם.** כָּל מַאֲכָל בְּמַשְׁמַע, כְּמוֹ ״נַשְׁחִיתָה עֵץ בְּלַחְמוֹ״ (ירמיהו יא:יט):


**Who will eat food.** All food is included [in לֶחֶם], as in, "Let us destroy the tree with its fruit ]בְּלַחְמוֹ[."¹²

footnotes:
¹² Yirmiyahu 11:19.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:25:1)


**וַיְהִי דְבַשׁ.** דְּבַשׁ קָנִים גָּדֵל בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**There was honey.** Cane sugar grows in Eretz Yisroel.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:26:1)


**וְהִנֵּה הָלַךְ דְּבַשׁ.** שֶׁהָיָה נוֹטֵף מִן הַקָּנִים:


**And behold there was a flow of honey.** Which was flowing from the canes.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:27:1)


**בְּיַעְרַת הַדְּבַשׁ.** בִּקְנֵה הַדְּבַשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״וַתָּשֶׂם בַּסּוּף״ (שמות ב:ג), דִּמְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״וְשַׁוִּיתֵיהּ בְּיַעְרָא״. וְ״סוּף״ הֵם קָנִים שֶׁל אֲגַם, וְכֵן ״אָכַלְתִּי יַעְרִי עִם דִּבְשִׁי״ (שיר השירים ה:א). וּבְלָשׁוֹן יִשְׁמְעֵאל קוֹרִין לְאוֹתוֹ דְּבַשׁ סוקר״א בְּלַעַ״ז, מִפִּי רַבִּי נָתָן הַיִּשְׁמְעֵאלִי:


**Into the honeycomb.** Into the sugar cane, as in, "and she placed it in the reeds,"¹³ which Onkelos renders, "and she placed it in the יַעֲרָא," and סוּף are reeds which grow in the swamp. And similarly, "I ate my sugar cane [יַעְרִי] with my sugar,¹⁴ and in Arabic, that honey is called *'sukra'* in their language; from R. Nosson Ha'yishme'eili.

footnotes:
¹³ Shemos 2:3.
¹⁴ Shir Hashirim 5:1.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:29:1)


**עָכַר אָבִי אֶת הָאָרֶץ.** בִּלְבֵּל אֶת דַּעְתָּם וְאֶת יְשׁוּעָתָם, כְּמַיִם עֲכוּרִים:


**My father has brought distress to the [inhabitants of the] land.** He confused [עָכַר] their thoughts and their salvation, like unclear [עֲכוּרִים] waters.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:30:1)


**כִּי עַתָּה לֹא רָבְתָה מַכָּה.** בִּתְמִיהָּה:


**Would it not now have been even greater—the blow.** This is a question.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:32:1)


**וַיַּעַשׂ הָעָם.** כְּמוֹ וַיַּעַט הָעָם, לְשׁוֹן עַיִט (בראשית טו:יא):


**The people rushed.** Meaning 'the people rushed,'¹⁵ an expression of ,¹⁶עַיִט [birds of prey [הָעַיִט] who swoop down].¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁵ According to the קְרִי; however, according to the כְּתִיב the meaning is “the people prepared.”
¹⁶ See below 25:14.
¹⁷ Bereishis 15:11.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:32:2)


**צֹאן וּבָקָר וּבְנֵי בָקָר.** אוֹמֵר אֲנִי: זֶה ״אֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ לֹא תִשְׁחֲטוּ״ (ויקרא כב:כח), וּלְכָךְ קְרָאָם חוֹטְאִים. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ בִּשְׁחִיטַת קָדָשִׁים, שֶׁהָיוּ מַקְדִּישִׁים אוֹתָם שְׁלָמִים, וְאוֹכְלִים לִפְנֵי זְרִיקַת דָּם, הוּא שֶׁאָמַר: וַיֹּאכַל הָעָם עַל הַדָּם, אוֹכְלִים קָדָשִׁים וַעֲדַיִן דָּם בַּמִּזְרָק:


**Sheep, cattle and their young.** I say that this was in violation of, "It and its young you must not slaughter [on the same day],"¹⁸ and he, therefore, called them sinners.¹⁹ But our Rabbis said in [Perek] שְׁחִיטַת קָדָשִׁים [in Maseches Zevochim]²⁰ that they sanctified them for peace-offerings, from which they were eating before the sprinkling of the blood. This is what Scripture [means when it] states, "And the people ate with the blood," i.e., they ate of the sacrifice when the blood was yet in the basin.²¹

footnotes:
¹⁸ Vayikra 22:28.
¹⁹ Below verse 33.
²⁰ 120a.
²¹ This violates the prohibition “You shall not eat over the blood” stated in Vayikra 19:26.—Sanhedrin 63a



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:33:1)


**גֹּלּוּ אֵלַי.** לְשׁוֹן גַּלְּלוּ:


**Roll over to me.** An expression of גַּלְּלוּ [roll].


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:33:2)


**הַיּוֹם.** מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם, וּלְמַטָּה (פסוק לד) נֶאֱמַר: ״וַיַּגִּישׁוּ אִישׁ שׁוֹרוֹ בְּיָדוֹ הַלַּיְלָה״, רַבּוֹתֵינוּ נֶחְלְקוּ בַּדָּבָר בְּסוֹף מַסֶּכֶת זְבָחִים, יֵשׁ מֵהֶן פֵּרְשׁוּ: כָּאן בְּחֻלִּין כָּאן בְּקָדָשִׁים. וְיֵשׁ מֵהֶן פֵּרְשׁוּ: כָּאן בְּקָדָשִׁים שֶׁהֻקְדְּשׁוּ לִקָּרֵב בְּבָמַת נוֹב גְּדוֹלָה, כָּאן בְּקָדָשִׁים שֶׁהֻקְדְּשׁוּ לִקָּרֵב בְּבָמָה קְטַנָּה, לִמֵּד עַל עוֹלַת יָחִיד שֶׁכְּשֵׁרָה לִשָּׁחֵט בַּלַּיְלָה:


**Today.** While it is yet daytime, and below it stated, "and all the people brought near, each man his ox in his hand that night."²² Our Rabbis had different views at the end of Maseches Zevochim;²³ some of them explain that [Scripture is referring to] ordinary animals,²⁴ while here²⁵ [the Scripture is referring to] sacrificial animals. Others explained that here²⁶ [Scripture is referring to] sacrificial animals which were sanctified to be offered at the large high place in Nov, while here²⁷ [Scripture is referring to] sacrificial animals which were sanctified to be offered at a small high place. Scripture thereby teaches us that a private burnt-offering may be slaughtered at night [on a small, i.e., private high place].

footnotes:
²² Below, verse 34.
²³ 120a.
²⁴ Below, verse 34.
²⁵ Verse 33.
²⁶ Ibid.
²⁷ Verse 34.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:33:3)


**אֶבֶן גְּדוֹלָה.** עֲשָׂאָהּ בָּמָה, לִזְרוֹק עָלֶיהָ דָּם וְהֶקְטֵר חֲלָבִים:


**A boulder.** He made it a high place upon which to sprinkle the blood and for the burning of the fatty parts.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:34:1)


**פֻּצוּ בָעָם.** הַכְרִיזוּ בִּמְקוֹמוֹת הַרְבֵּה, וְיִהְיוּ הַמַּכְרִיזִים נְפוֹצִים בָּעָם:


**Spread out among the people.** Announce in many places, and let the announcers disperse among the people.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:34:2)


**וּשְׁחַטְתֶּם בָּזֶה.** כָּאן, בִּמְקוֹם שְׁחִיטָה. דָּבָר אַחֵר: סַכִּין בָּדַק לָהֶם:


**And you shall slaughter [them] here.** Here in the place of slaughtering.²⁸ Another explanation is, he examined a slaughtering knife²⁹ for them.³⁰

footnotes:
²⁸ בָּזֶה referring to the “place” of slaughtering.
²⁹ בָּזֶה referring to the “knife” used for slaughtering. He had inspected it to make sure it was fit for ritual slaughter.
³⁰ Maseches Chulin 17b.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:34:3)


**הַלַּיְלָה.** בְּהֵמוֹת שֶׁל חֻלִּין נִשְׁחֲטוּ בַּלַּיְלָה, כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בִּזְבָחִים (שם):


**That night.** The ordinary [i.e., non-sanctified] animals were slaughtered at night. Thus it is explained in [Maseches] Zevochim.³¹

footnotes:
³¹ 120a.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:35:1)


**אֹתוֹ הֵחֵל.** אוֹתוֹ הַמִּזְבֵּחַ, רִאשׁוֹן לַמִּזְבְּחוֹת שֶׁבָּנָה שָׁאוּל:


**It was the first of the altars.** That altar was the first of the altars which Shaul built.³²

footnotes:
³² He built this altar in honor of the victory, as Moshe did after having defeated Amoleik in Shemos 17:15.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:38:1)


**פִּנּוֹת הָעָם.** שָׂרֵי הָעָם, אֶבֶן הַפִּנָּה הִיא הַחֲשׁוּבָה:


**Leaders of the people.** The chiefs of the people; the cornerstone אֶבֶן הַפִּנָּה is the most esteemed.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:40:1)


**לְעֵבֶר אֶחָד.** לְהַפִּיל גּוֹרָל בֵּין כֻּלְּכֶם וּבֵינֵינוּ:


**On one side.** To cast lots between all of you, and us.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:41:1)


**הָבָה תָמִים.** תֵּן גּוֹרָל אֱמֶת:


**Make it truthful.** Give a true lot.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:41:2)


**וְהָעָם יָצָאוּ.** נְקִיִּים מִן הַגּוֹרָל:


**And the people were excluded.** Innocent, from the lot.



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:45:1)


**וַיִּפְדּוּ הָעָם.** הִתִּירוּ לוֹ לְשָׁאוּל שְׁבוּעָתוֹ:


**The people redeemed [Yonoson].** They nullified Shaul's oath.



### Verse 48

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:48:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:48:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:48:1)


**וַיַּעַשׂ חָיִל.** וַיֶּאֱסֹף חַיִל:


**He raised an army.** And he gathered an army.



### Verse 50

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:50:1)


**אֲבִינֵר בֶּן נֵר.** וְנֵר הָיָה דּוֹד שָׁאוּל, אָחִיו שֶׁל קִישׁ, וּשְׁנֵיהֶם בְּנֵי אֲבִיאֵל, וְגַם אֲבִיאֵל נִקְרָא נֵר, בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים (א ח:לג), שֶׁהָיָה מַדְלִיק נֵרוֹת לָרַבִּים בַּמְּבוֹאוֹת הָאֲפֵלִים (ויקרא רבה ט:ב):


**Avineir the son of Neir.** Neir was Shaul's uncle, the brother of Kish, and both were the sons of Avieil. Aviei was also known as Neir, in Divrei Hayomim,³³ because he would light candles for the public in the dark streets.³⁴

footnotes:
³³ I Divrei Hayomim 8:33.
³⁴ See Radak above 9:1.



### Verse 52

###### Rashi on I Samuel 14:52:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:52:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/14:52:1)


**וְרָאָה שָׁאוּל.** לְשׁוֹן הֹוֶה, כְּשֶׁהָיָה שָׁאוּל רוֹאֶה אִישׁ גִּבּוֹר חַיִל, הָיָה אוֹסְפוֹ אֵלָיו:


**And whenever Shaul saw.** This is in the present tense, meaning, when Shaul would see a valiant soldier, he would bring him [into his army].



## Chapter 15


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:1:1)


**וְעַתָּה שְׁמַע לְקוֹל דִּבְרֵי ה׳.** פַּעַם אַחַת נִסְכַּלְתָּ, עַתָּה הִזָּהֵר בְּעַצְמְךָ:


**Now obey Adonoy's words.** Once you acted foolishly,¹ but now be careful [in your actions].

footnotes:
¹ Shaul had acted improperly by not waiting for Shmuel [Above 13:8-14] as he had been instructed, therefore, Shmuel is warning him now to be careful.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:3:1)


**מִשּׁוֹר וְעַד שֶׂה.** שֶׁהָיוּ בַּעֲלֵי כְּשָׁפִים, וּמְשַׁנִּין עַצְמָן וְדוֹמִין לִבְהֵמָה:


**Oxen and sheep.** They were sorcerers, and they would change themselves to appear as animals.²

footnotes:
² Radak suggests another reason for destroying the animals. The Torah specifies that the memory of Amoleik should be erased. Therefore, Shmuel instructed that not even an ox or a sheep should remain about which one might say ‘this animal belonged to Amoleik.’



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:4:1)


**וַיְשַׁמַּע שָׁאוּל.** לְשׁוֹן הַכְרָזָה, כְּמוֹ (שמואל א יד:כ): ״וַיִּזָּעֵק שָׁאוּל״:


**Shaul summoned.** An expression of announcing [meaning the same] as in .³וַיִזָּעֵק שָׁאוּל

footnotes:
³ Above 14:20.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:4:2)


**וַיִּפְקְדֵם בַּטְּלָאִים.** אָמַר לְכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד, שֶׁיִּקַּח טָלֶה מִצֹּאנוֹ שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ מָנָה אֶת הַטְּלָאִים, לְפִי שֶׁאָסוּר לִמְנוֹת אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בָּם (בראשית לב:יב): ״לֹא יִסָּפֵר מֵרֹב״:


**And he counted them with lambs.** He told each one to take a lamb⁴ from the king's flocks, and afterwards he counted the lambs, because it is forbidden to count Jewish people.⁵ for it is stated concerning them, '[which] are too numerous to count.'⁶

footnotes:
⁴ According to Targum this occurred just before Pesach, and the lambs were Pascal sacrifices.
⁵ See Maseches Yoma 22b which states that in order to avoid counting the Bnei Yisroel directly which is forbidden by the Torah, each man took a lamb from the king’s flock and the lambs were then counted. See also Kli Yokor in Bamidbar 1:2 who discusses the exceptions to this rule.
⁶ Bereishis 32:12.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:5:1)


**וַיָּרֶב בַּנָּחַל.** וַיִּלָּחֵם בַּנַּחַל וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (יומא כב ב): עַל עִסְקֵי נַחַל רָב וְדָן אֶת עַצְמוֹ, וּמָה בִּשְׁבִיל נֶפֶשׁ אַחַת אָמְרָה תּוֹרָה עֲרוֹף עֶגְלָה בַּנַּחַל, בִּשְׁבִיל כָּל הַנְּפָשׁוֹת הָאֵלּוּ לֹא כָּל שֶׁכֵּן, אִם אָדָם חָטָא, בְּהֵמָה מֶה חָטְאָה:


**And he fought in the valley.** [Meaning the same as] וַיִלָּחֵם בַּנַּחַל [=and he fought in the valley]. Our Rabbis said, concerning the valley, he argued and concluded, that if for the sake of one life, the Torah said to decapitate a calf in the valley,⁷ how much more for the sake of all these lives.⁸ If man has sinned, what have the animals sinned?

footnotes:
⁷ See Devarim 21:4.
⁸ Shaul rationalized that he will spare the animals to use as sacrifices to atone for the Amoleikites that were killed. For additional arguments presented by Shaul and their consequences, see Maseches Yoma 22b.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:6:1)


**אֶל הַקֵּינִי.** בְּנֵי יִתְרוֹ, שֶׁהָיוּ יוֹשְׁבִין בְּמִדְבַּר יְהוּדָה בְּנֶגֶב עֲרָד, שֶׁהָלְכוּ אֵצֶל יַעְבֵּץ לִלְמוֹד תּוֹרָה, וַעֲרָד הוּא עֲמָלֵק, הַכְּנַעֲנִי מֶלֶךְ עֲרָד, עֲמָלֵק הָיָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במדבר כא:א): ״יוֹשֵׁב הַנֶּגֶב״, וַ״עֲמָלֵק יוֹשֵׁב בְּאֶרֶץ הַנֶּגֶב״ (במדבר יג:כט):


**To the Kenites.** Who were Yisro's descendants,⁹ who were residing in the wilderness of Yehudah, in the south of Arod, who went to Ya'aveitz to learn Torah, Arod being Amoleik. The Canaanite king of Arod was Amoleik, for it is stated [about him]; "who lived in the South,"¹⁰ and, "Amoleik dwells in the southern part of the land."¹¹

footnotes:
⁹ See Shoftim 1:16.
¹⁰ Bamidbar 21:1.
¹¹ Ibid., 13:29.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:6:2)


**עִם כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** שֶׁנֶּהֱנוּ מִסְּעוּדָתוֹ מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן וְכָל זִקְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, וַהֲרֵי מַעֲלֶה עָלָיו כְּאִלּוּ עָשָׂה חֶסֶד עִם כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**To all the Bnei Yisroel.** Who partook from his [Yisro's] meal, Moshe, Aharon and all the elders of Yisroel.¹² It is considered as though he acted kindly with all the Bnei Yisroel.¹³

footnotes:
¹² See Shemos 18:12.
¹³ It may also refer to Yisro’s hospitality of welcoming Moshe into his home when he escaped from Paroh [Shemos 2:20-21]. Based on this event R. Nechemyah in Maseches Berachos 63b states ‘how great is the reward for one who invites a Torah scholar into his house, gives him to eat and drink, and shares his possessions with him.’ Others believe that the act of kindness with all the Bnei Yisroel refers to the good advice that Yisro gave to Moshe with regard to setting up a system of judges [Shemos 18:14-26], because this benefitted the entire nation.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:9:1)


**וְהַמִּשְׁנִים.** אֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן, וְהֵם הַבְּרִיאִים וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי: לְשׁוֹן ׳מִשְׁנִים׳, כְּמוֹ (בראשית מג:יב): ״כֶּסֶף מִשְׁנֶה״, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהוּא כָּפוּל בְּבָשָׂר וּבְשׁוּמָן, וְכֵן (שופטים ו:כה): ״וּפַר הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁבַע שָׁנִים״:


**The fattened cattle.** This word has no likeness [in Scripture]. These are the fattened cattle;¹⁴ and I say that the expression of מִשְׁנִים is like וְכֶסֶף מִשְׁנֶה [meaning double money],¹⁵ because they are doubled over with flesh and fat. Similarly, 'And the ox which was fattened [הַשֵּׁנִי] seven years.'¹⁶

footnotes:
¹⁴ According to Mahri Kra מִשֽׁנִים are thoroughbred horses.
¹⁵ Bereishis 43:12.
¹⁶ Shoftim 6:25.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:9:2)


**נִמְבְזָה וְנָמֵס.** כְּמוֹ נִבְזֶה, וְהַמֵּ״ם טְפֵלָה בּוֹ:


**Undesirable and inferior.** Meaning the same as נִבְזֶה; and the 'מ' is superfluous.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:11:1)


**נִחַמְתִּי.** מַחֲשָׁבָה עָלְתָה בְּלִבִּי עַל מַלְכוּת שָׁאוּל שֶׁהִמְלַכְתִּי:


**I have reconsidered.** A thought has risen in my heart concerning Shaul's kingship, whom I have appointed king.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:12:1)


**מַצִּיב לוֹ יָד.** בּוֹנֶה לוֹ מִזְבֵּחַ הוּא הַמִּזְבֵּחַ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּאֵלִיָּהוּ (מלכים א יח:ל): ״וַיְרַפֵּא אֶת מִזְבַּח ה׳ הֶהָרוּס״. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וְהָא מְתַקֵּן לֵיהּ תַּמָּן אֲתַר לְפַלְגָא בֵּיהּ בִּיזְתָא״:


**And is setting up a place for himself.** He is building an altar for himself. That is the altar referred to concerning Eliyohu, 'he repaired the ruined altar of Adonoy.'¹⁷ Targum Yonoson renders, 'and he is making room there for himself to divide the spoils.'

footnotes:
¹⁷ Melachim I 18:30.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:16:1)


**הֶרֶף.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״אוֹרִיךְ״, פֵּירוּשׁ הַמְתֵּן:


**Stay.** Like the Targum, אוֹרִיךְ meaning 'wait.'



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:17:1)


**רֹאשׁ שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אָתָּה.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״שִׁבְטָא דְבִנְיָמִין עֲבַר בְּיַמָּא בְּרֵישׁ כָּל עַמָּא״, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (תהלים סח:כח): ״בִּנְיָמִין צָעִיר רֹדֵם״:


**You are the head of the tribes of Yisroel.** Targum Yonoson renders, 'the tribe of Binyomin crossed the [Reed] Sea ahead of all the people, as it is said, 'Binyomin, the youngest, rules them.'¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ Tehillim 68:28. You did not follow in Binyomin’s tradition of taking charge and leading the people without questioning the command of ‘ה. You have thus forfeited the merit by which you became king.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:18:1)


**עַד כַּלּוֹתָם אוֹתָם.** הָעָם אֲשֶׁר אִתְּךָ יְכַלּוּ אוֹתָם:


**Until they destroy them.** The people who are with you will destroy them.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:19:1)


**וַתַּעַט.** כְּמוֹ וַתָּעָף, לְשׁוֹן עַיִט (בראשית טו:יא):


**You rushed.** Meaning 'וַתַּעַף' [you flew], an expression related to 'עַיִט' [birds of prey].¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ Bereishis 15:11.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:21:1)


**רֵאשִׁית הַחֵרֶם.** מֵיטַב הַחֵרֶם, וְכֵן (דברים יח:ד): ״רֵאשִׁית דְּגָנְךָ״, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במדבר יח:ל): ״בַּהֲרִימְכֶם אֶת חֶלְבּוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ״. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״קֳדָם דְּיַחְרְמוּן״:


**The best of that which was to be destroyed.** [רֵאשִׁית] meaning the best of the ,²⁰חֵרֶם and similarly, "the best of your corn,"²¹ as it is stated, "when you set aside the finest part of it."²² Targum Yonoson renders [רֵאשִׁית] 'before they banned it.'

footnotes:
²⁰ Perhaps by his statement Shaul was indicating to Shmuel that they examined the animals to ascertain that none were Amoleikite sorcerers who had changed themselves to appear as animals [See above verse 2].—Da’as Sofrim
²¹ Devarim 18:4.
²² Bamidbar 18:30.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:22:1)


**הִנֵּה שְׁמֹעַ.** בְּקוֹלוֹ, טוֹב מִזֶּבַח:


**Behold, obeying** His command is better than a choice offering.²³

footnotes:
²³ ‘ה prefers the fulfillment of His command over the offering of voluntary sacrifices.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:22:2)


**לְהַקְשִׁיב מֵחֵלֶב אֵילִים.** זֶה מוּסָב עַל הִנֵּה שְׁמֹעַ מִזֶּבַח טוֹב, וּלְהַקְשִׁיב, מֵחֵלֶב אֵילִים:


**To hearken [is better] than the fat of rams.** This refers to, "Behold, obeying [is better] than a choice offering, and to hearken [is better] than the fat of rams."



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:23:1)


**כִּי חַטַּאת קֶסֶם מֶרִי וְאָוֶן וּתְרָפִים הַפְצַר.** וּכְעֹנֶשׁ שֶׁל אָוֶן וּתְרָפִים, כֵּן עֹנֶשׁ שֶׁל הַפְצַר וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״אֲרֵי כְחוֹב גַּבְרַיָּא וְכוּ׳ וּכְחוֹבֵי עַמָּא דְטָעוּ בָּתַר טָעוּתָא, כֵּן חוֹבַת כָּל אֱנָשׁ דְּפָצַר וּמוֹסִיף וְכוּ׳״:


**For defiance is like the sin of sorcery, and stubborness is like the sin of idolatry.** And like the penalty for idolatry, so is the penalty for stubborness.²⁴ Targum Yonoson renders, 'For, as the sin of a people [who divine, so is the sin of any man who disobeys the word of Adonoy]²⁵ and as the sins of a nation who has strayed after idols, so is the sin of any person who adds [to the words of a prophet].

footnotes:
²⁴ Shaul’s original sin was compared to sorcery, but his insisting on his innocence was compared to the more serious sin of idolatry; Shaul was compounding his original sin by insisting on his innocence.
²⁵ Just as one who divines demonstrates that he removes his trust in ‘ה and looks for other means to determine his future, so does one who disobeys and rebels against the word of ‘ה indicate he removes his belief in the power of ‘ה to reward and punish.—Radak


###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:23:2)


**הַפְצַר.** לְשׁוֹן תּוֹסֶפֶת, וְכֵן (בראשית לג:יא): ״וַיִּפְצַר בּוֹ״, הִרְבָּה עָלָיו דְּבָרִים:


**Stubborness.** An expression adding, and similarly, "He urged [וַיִפְצַר] him,"²⁶ i.e., he spoke many words to convince him.

footnotes:
²⁶ Bereishis 33:11.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:24:1)


**יָרֵאתִי אֶת הָעָם.** דּוֹאֵג הָאֲדוֹמִי, שֶׁהָיָה חָשׁוּב כֻּלָּם:


**I feared the people.** [Referring to] Do'eg, the Edomite,²⁷ who was as important as all of them.

footnotes:
²⁷ He was the head of the Sanhedrin and was considered a giant in his Torah scholarship. See Maseches Sanhedrin 106b.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:27:1)


**וַיַּחֲזֵק בִּכְנַף מְעִילוֹ.** לְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ מַשְׁמָעוֹ, שֶׁכְּשֶׁפָּנָה שְׁמוּאֵל לָלֶכֶת מֵאַחֲרֵי שָׁאוּל, אָחַז שָׁאוּל בְּכָנָף שֶׁל שְׁמוּאֵל, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה שָׁאוּל מְבַקֵּשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ שֶׁיָּשׁוּב עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁתַּחֲוֶה בַּגִּלְגָּל שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (שוחר טוב) חוֹלְקִים אֲמוֹרָאִים, יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: מְעִילוֹ שֶׁל שְׁמוּאֵל קָרַע שָׁאוּל. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: מְעִילוֹ שֶׁל שָׁאוּל קָרַע שְׁמוּאֵל, וּמָסַר לוֹ סִימָן זֶה, מִי שֶׁיִּכְרוֹת כְּנַף מְעִילוֹ, הוּא יִמְלוֹךְ תַּחְתָּיו, וְהוּא שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ שָׁאוּל לְדָוִד בַּיּוֹם שֶׁכָּרַת אֶת הַמְּעִיל (שמואל א כד:כ): ״יָדַעְתִּי כִּי מָלוֹךְ תִּמְלוֹךְ״:


**And he grabbed the corner of his robe.** According to the simple meaning, it seems that when Shmuel turned to leave Shaul, Shaul grabbed the corner of Shmuel's [robe], since Shaul was asking him to return until he would prostate himself in Gilgol, where the Tent of Meeting was. In Midrash Aggadah, however, Amora'im differ; some say that Shaul tore Shmuel's robe, and some say that Shmuel tore Shaul's robe,²⁸ giving over to him this sign, that whoever will tear a corner of his robe, would reign in his place. And that is what Shaul said to Dovid on the day he tore [Shaul's] robe, 'I know that you will surely reign.'²⁹

footnotes:
²⁸ As a symbol of his loss of kingship. Another version is that Shmuel tore his own robe as a gesture of dismay.
²⁹ Below 24:21. See Rashi’s commentary there.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:29:1)


**וְגַם נֵצַח יִשְׂרָאֵל לֹא יְשַׁקֵּר.** וְאִם תֹּאמַר אָשׁוּב מֵעֲוֹנִי לְפָנָיו, לֹא יוֹעִיל עוֹד לִטּוֹל אֶת הַמְּלוּכָה מִמִּי שֶׁנִּתְּנָה לוֹ, כִּי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהוּא נִצְחוֹנוֹ שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, לֹא יְשַׁקֵּר מִלִּתֵּן הַטּוֹבָה לָזֶה שֶׁאָמַר לִתֵּן:


**But, the Eternal One of Yisroel neither lies.** And if you will say, 'I will repent of my sin before Him,' it will no longer avail to take the kingship from the one to whom it was given, for the Holy One, Blessed is He, Who is the Eternal One of Yisroel, will not lie by not giving the good to whom He said to give it.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:32:1)


**אָגַג מַעֲדַנּוֹת.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״אֲגַג מְפַנְּקָא״:


**Agag went to him bound.** Targum Yonoson renders, mincingly.³⁰

footnotes:
³⁰ Malbim renders ‘joyfully.’


###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:32:2)


**אָכֵן סָר מַר הַמָּוֶת.** בֶּאֱמֶת יָדַעְתִּי כִּי סָר וְקָרֵב עָלַי מְרִירַת הַמָּוֶת:


**Indeed, the bitterness of death approaches.** Indeed, I know that it has turned and is approaching toward me—the bitterness of death.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:33:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר שִׁכְּלָה נָשִׁים חַרְבֶּךָ.** לְהוֹשִׁיבָם אַלְמָנוֹת מִבַּעֲלֵיהֶם, שֶׁהֱיִיתֶם חוֹתְכִים זַנְבֵי בַּחוּרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**As your sword bereaved women.** To render them widows from their husbands, for you would cut off the membrum virile of the youths of Yisroel.³¹

footnotes:
³¹ See Rashi Devarim 25:18.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 15:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/15:33:2)


**וַיְשַׁסֵּף.** חִתְּכוֹ לְאַרְבַּע, וְאֵין לַתֵּיבָה זוֹ דִּמְיוֹן וְתַרְגּוּם וַיְשַׁסֵּף, ״וּפָשַׁח״. וּבַגְּמָרָא שֶׁלָּנוּ יֵשׁ הֲוָה מְפַשַּׁח וְיָהִיב לָן אֲלוּתָא, כְּלוֹמַר בּוֹקֵעַ:


**[Shmuel] cut.** He cut him into four pieces. This word has no likeness [in Scripture]. The Targum renders וַיְשַׁסֵּף as וּפָשַׁח. And in our Gemara we find, 'he broke off [מפשח] a branch, and gave us several twigs,'³² meaning that he split.³³

footnotes:
³² Beitzah 33b.
³³ See Rashi’s commentary in Eichah 3:11.



## Chapter 16


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:1:1)


**בֵּית הַלַּחְמִי.** בְּבֵית לֶחֶם, וְכֵן דֶּרֶךְ כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ בִּשְׁתֵּי תֵיבוֹת, כְּמוֹ: בֵּית לֶחֶם, בֵּית שֶׁמֶשׁ, בֵּית אֵל, קִרְיַת אַרְבַּע, אִם בָּא לְהָטִיל בּוֹ הֵ״א, מֵטִיל בֵּין שְׁתֵּי הַתֵּיבוֹת, כְּגוֹן: קִרְיַת הָאַרְבַּע, בֵּית הָאֵלִי, בֵּית הַלַּחְמִי:


**The Beis Lechemite.** From Beis Lechem. The rule of every noun composed of two words, e.g., Beis Lechem, Beis Shemesh, Beis Eil, Kiryas Arba, if one wishes to add a 'ה' he adds it between the two words, e.g., Kiryas Ha'arba,¹ Beis Ha'Eili,² Beis Ha'lachmi.

footnotes:
¹ Bereishis 35:27.
² I Melachim 16:34.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:4:1)


**וַיֶּחֶרְדוּ.** מִהֲרוּ לָצֵאת לִקְרָאתוֹ:


**Hurried.** Hurried out toward him.³

footnotes:
³ The elders of the city who saw Shmuel approaching hurried out toward him thinking that they had committed a grave sin and that was why he was coming to their city. Therefore, Shmuel was asked whether he was coming in peace.—Radak



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:6:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר אַךְ נֶגֶד ה׳ מְשִׁיחוֹ.** וַיֹּאמֶר בְּלִבּוֹ, אַךְ אֱמֶת הוּא זֶה:


**He said, "Surely, before Adonoy is His annointed."** He said to himself, 'Surely, it is true that this one is fitting for the kingship.'



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:7:1)


**אַל תַּבֵּט אֶל מַרְאֵהוּ.** אֶל יוֹפִי תָּאֳרוֹ:


**Do not look at his appearance.** Upon the beauty of his stature.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:7:2)


**כִּי מְאַסְתִּיהוּ.** לְפִי שֶׁכַּעְסָן הוּא, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שמואל א יז:כח): ״וַיִּחַר אַף אֱלִיאָב בְּדָוִד״:


**For I have rejected him.** Because he is a quick tempered person, as it is stated, 'And Eliov became angry with Dovid.'⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Below 17:28. Also, see Maseches Pesachim 66b, that Eliov actually possessed the qualities of a king but ‘ה rejected him because he had a bad temper.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:7:3)


**כִּי לֹא אֲשֶׁר יִרְאֶה הָאָדָם.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁקָּרָאתָ לְעַצְמְךָ רוֹאֶה, שֶׁאָמַרְתָּ לְשָׁאוּל (שמואל א ט:יט): ״אָנֹכִי הָרוֹאֶה״, כָּאן אֲנִי מוֹדִיעֲךָ שֶׁאֵינְךָ רוֹאֶה:


**For it is not as man perceives it.** Even though you called yourself a seer, when you said to Shaul, 'I am the seer,'⁵ here I am informing you that you do not see.

footnotes:
⁵ Above 9:19. ה׳ punished Shmuel for inferring albeit an infinitesimal of pride by saying “I am the seer.” Moshe was similarly punished for saying “the matter that will be too difficult for you, present to me and I will hear it.” [Devarim 1:17]—Sifre, Devarim 1:17



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:9:1)


**וַיַּעֲבֵר יִשַׁי שַׁמָּה.** הוּא שַׁמְעָא:


**Yishay brought Shammoh.** That is שַׁמְעָא.⁶

footnotes:
⁶ I.e.	שַׁמְעָא = שַׁמׇּה See II Shmuel 21:21 and I Divrei Hayomim 2:13.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:11:1)


**שָׁאַר הַקָּטָן.** נִשְׁאַר הַקָּטָן:


**The youngest one is still left.** The youngest one is left.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:11:2)


**לֹא נָסֹב.** לֹא נֵשֵׁב לֶאֱכוֹל, כָּל מוֹשַׁב סְעוּדָה קְרוּיָה הֲסִבָּה:


**We will not sit.** We will not sit to eat; every instance of sitting for a meal is called הֲסִבׇּה [leaning].



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:13:1)


**וַתִּצְלַח רוּחַ ה׳.** רוּחַ גְּבוּרָה:


**And a spirit of Adonoy passed over.** A spirit of strength.⁷

footnotes:
⁷ Targum renders ‘a spirit of prophecy.’



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:16:1)


**עֲבָדֶיךָ לְפָנֶיךָ.** הִנֵּה עֲבָדֶיךָ לְפָנֶיךָ אֲשֶׁר יַעֲשׂוּ מִצְוֹתֶיךָ:


**Your servants who are before you.** Behold, your servants are before you, who will carry out your orders.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:18:1)


**אֶחָד מֵהַנְּעָרִים.** מְיֻחָד שֶׁבָּהֶם, דּוֹאֵג הָאֲדוֹמִי:


**One of the youths.** The special one, [i.e.,] Do'eg, the Edomite.⁸

footnotes:
⁸ See Maseches Sanhedrin 93b.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 16:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/16:18:2)


**יוֹדֵעַ נַגֵּן.** כָּל עַצְמוֹ נִתְכַּוֵּן לְהַכְנִיס עַיִן רָעָה שֶׁל שָׁאוּל בְּדָוִד, שֶׁיִּתְקַנֵּא בּוֹ:


**Knows how to play.** His entire intention was to induce Shaul's evil eye into Dovid, that he envy him.⁹

footnotes:
⁹ See Ibid., which states that it was Do’eg’s intention to arouse Shaul’s envy and therefore he gave Dovid excessive praise which was irrelevant to the position for which Shaul needed him. Do’eg neither described Dovid’s musical talents nor the instrument that he played.



## Chapter 17


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:1:1)


**בְּאֶפֶס דַּמִּים.** כָּךְ שֵׁם הַמָּקוֹם:


**At Ephes Damim.** This was the name of the place.¹

footnotes:
¹ It literally means ‘no more blood’ because it was there that the bloodshed between the Bnei Yisroel and the Philistines ceased.—Radak



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:4:1)


**אִישׁ הַבֵּנַיִם.** גִּבּוֹר לֵיצֵא מִמַּעַרְכְתּוֹ, לַעֲמוֹד בֵּין שְׁתֵּי הַמַּעֲרָכוֹת:


**A champion.** A mighty person who went out from his battalion, to stand between the two battalions.²

footnotes:
² Following the literal meaning of אִישׁ הַבֵּנַיִם as ‘the man of between.’ However, Maseches Sotah 42b states that the word הַבֵּנַיִם is from the root בנה, to build, i.e., he was as sturdy as a building.—Da’as Sofrim



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:5:1)


**וְשִׁרְיוֹן קַשְׂקַשִּׂים.** כְּמִין קַשְׂקַשּׂוֹת דָּגִים עֲשׂוּיִן מִבַּרְזֶל, נוֹפְלִים עַל גַּב הַשִּׁרְיוֹן [ספרים אחרים נִקְבֵי הַשִּׁרְיוֹן], קַשְׂקֶשֶׂת לְכָל נֶקֶב:


**Scaled armor.** Like fish scales, made of iron, spread over the coat³ (another version: the holes of the coat), a scale [covering] each hole.

footnotes:
³ Not only was Golyos impressive, but his armor also appeared impregnable.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:6:1)


**וּמִצְחַת נְחֹשֶׁת.** כְּמִין יַד בַּרְזֶל הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַכּוֹבַע כְּנֶגֶד הַחוֹטִים, שֶׁקּוֹרִין נש״ל בלע״ז, וְהָיְתָה מַגַּעַת לוֹ עַד רַגְלָיו:


**Copper shields.** A sort of iron handle, extending from the helmet, opposite the nose, called *nasal* in O.F., and it reached his feet.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:6:2)


**וְכִידוֹן נְחֹשֶׁת.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּמְסַחֲפָא דִנְחָשָׁא נָפֵיק מִן קוֹלְסָא וּמְטַל בֵּין כַּתְפוֹהִי״. פֵּירוּשׁ: כְּמִין חֲנִית נְחֹשֶׁת בּוֹלֶטֶת מִן הַכּוֹבַע, וְנִמְשֶׁכֶת בֵּין כְּתֵפָיו, לְהָגֵן עַל צַוָּארוֹ מִן הַחֶרֶב:


**A copper guard.** Targum Yonoson renders, וּמְסַחֲפָא דִּנְחָשָׁא נָפֵיק מִן קוֹלְסָא וּמְטַל בֵּין כַּתְפוֹהִי, meaning, 'a sort of copper javelin, protruding from the helmet, and extending between his shoulders,' to protect his neck from [injury by] the sword.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Others translate ‘a copper javelin slung between his shoulders’ because he held other weapons in his hands.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:7:1)


**כִּמְנוֹר אֹרְגִים.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״כְּאַכְסָן דְּגַרְדָּאִין״, אינשובל״א בלע״ז:


**Like a weaver's beam.** Targum Yonoson renders, 'like a weaver's beam', *ensouble* in O.F.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:7:2)


**שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת שְׁקָלִים.** כָּל הַשְּׁקָלִים הָאֲמוּרִים בִּנְבִיאִים, מָנִים:


**Six hundred shekel.** All *shekels* mentioned in the *Nevi'im,* are 'maneh.'



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8:1)


**לָמָּה תֵצְאוּ.** כֻּלְּכֶם לַעֲרוֹךְ מִלְחָמָה, יִלָּחֵם אֶחָד כְּנֶגְדִּי בִּשְׁבִיל כֻּלְּכֶם, וּבִתְנַאי שֶׁהוּא מְפוֹרָשׁ: אִם יוּכַל לְהִלָּחֵם אִתִּי וְגוֹ׳:


**Why should you go out.** [Why should] all of you go out to wage war? Let one representing all of you fight against me, and on the condition specified, i.e., "if he will be able to fight me, etc."


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8:2)


**הֲלֹא אָנֹכִי הַפְּלִשְׁתִּי.** אֵינִי לֹא שַׂר מֵאָה וְלֹא שַׂר אֶלֶף, אֶלָּא פְלִשְׁתִּי כְּאֶחָד מִן הָאֲחֵרִים, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּלְחַמְתִּי לָהֶם כַּמָּה מִלְחָמוֹת, אֲנִי הָרַגְתִּי חָפְנִי וּפִינְחָס, וְשָׁבִיתִי אֶת הָאָרוֹן, וְאַתֶּם נַעֲשֵׂיתֶם עֲבָדִים לְשָׁאוּל, וְלֹא הוֹשִׁיעַ אֶתְכֶם מִיָּמָיו בִּגְבוּרָתוֹ:


**Am I not the Philistine.** I am neither an officer over a hundred, nor an officer over a thousand. I am a Philistine like one of the others, even though I have fought many wars for them. I killed Chofni and Pinchas, and I captured the Ark, while you have become servants to Shaul, and he never saved you with his might.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8:3)


**בְּרוּ לָכֶם.** בָּרְרוּ לָכֶם:


**Choose for yourselves.** Choose [בָּרְרוּ] for yourselves.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:8:4)


**אִישׁ.** אֶת שָׁאוּל, וְיֵרֵד אֵלָי:


**A man.** Shaul, and let him come down to me.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:12:1)


**אֶפְרָתִי.** בֵּית לֶחֶם יוֹשֶׁבֶת בְּאֶרֶץ אֶפְרָת:


**Ephrathite.** Beis Lechem is located in the land of Ephros.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ See Bereishis 35:19.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:12:2)


**וְלוֹ שְׁמֹנָה בָנִים.** וּמַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב (בדברי הימים א ב:טו): ״אֹצֶם הַשִּׁשִּׁי דָּוִד הַשְּׁבִיעִי״ וְלֹא כָתַב יוֹתֵר, יֵשׁ לוֹמַר שֶׁאֶחָד מֵת בְּלֹא בָּנִים, וּלְפִיכָךְ לֹא כְּתָבוֹ שָׁם:


**And he had eight sons.** And that which is stated, 'Otzem, the sixth, Dovid the seventh,'⁶ and no more is stated. This may be reconciled that one died childless and therefore, he was not mentioned there.

footnotes:
⁶ I Divrei Hayomim 2:15.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:12:3)


**בָּאֲנָשִׁים.** נִמְנֶה בִּכְנֵסִיּוֹת הַחֲשׁוּבִים:


**Among the elders.** Counted in the gatherings of important people.⁷

footnotes:
⁷ See Maseches Berachos 58a, which states that wherever  Yishay went he was accompanied by a large crowd.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:14:1)


**וְדָוִד הוּא הַקָּטָן.** שֶׁהָיָה בְּבֵית שָׁאוּל מִקֹּדֶם לָכֵן לְנַגֵּן לְפָנָיו:


**Dovid was the youngest.** Who had previously been in Shaul's palace to play music before him.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:15:1)


**וְדָוִד הֹלֵךְ.** הָיָה הוֹלֵךְ וָשָׁב תָּמִיד מֵאֵצֶל שָׁאוּל לְבֵית אָבִיו:


**Dovid would go.** Would always go back and forth from Shaul to his father's house.⁸

footnotes:
⁸ Dovid was not permanently assigned to be with Shaul. Dovid played music for Shaul when the evil spirit rested upon him. At all other times he would return home to tend his father’s sheep.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:16:1)


**הַשְׁכֵּם וְהַעֲרֵב.** אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (סוטה מב ב): כְּדֵי לְבַטְּלָם מִקְּרִיאַת שְׁמַע:


**Morning and evening.** Our Rabbis said, to prevent them from reciting 'shema.'⁹

footnotes:
⁹ See Maseches Sotah 42b.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:18:1)


**חֲרִיצֵי הֶחָלָב.** (תרגום:) ״גוּבְנִין דְּחַלְבָּא״:


**Cheeses.** [Targum Yonoson renders] 'cheeses.'


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:18:2)


**לְשַׂר הָאָלֶף.** אֲנִי אוֹמֵר: לְשַׂר שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ אֶלֶף שֶׁאֶחָיו (ספרים אחרים שֶׁאֲנִי) מִמֶּנּוּ, שֶׁלְּכָל הַשְּׁבָטִים הָיוּ שָׂרֵי אֲלָפִים לְפִי אַלְפֵיהֶם, וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי: לְשַׂר הָאָלֶף זֶה יְהוֹנָתָן, שֶׁאָמַר עָלָיו (שמואל א יג:ב): וְאֶלֶף הָיוּ עִם יוֹנָתָן בְּגִבְעַת בִּנְיָמִין:


**To the captain of the thousand.** I say 'to the captain of that thousand to which his brothers belong,' for all the tribes had captains of thousands according to their thousands. But I have heard, that 'to the captain of the thousand' refers to Yonoson, concerning whom [Scripture] stated, 'And a thousand were with Yonoson in Givas Binyomin'.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ Above 13:2.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:18:3)


**וְאֶת עֲרֻבָּתָם תִּקָּח.** וְאֶת עֲרוּבַת הַצָּלָתָם וּשְׁלוֹמָם, תִּקַּח בְּאָזְנֶיךָ וּבִלְבָבְךָ, וְתַגֵּד לִי וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְיַת טִיבֵיהוֹן תַּיְתִי״:


**And take the tidings of their welfare.** The assurance of their salvation and their welfare you shall take into your ears and to your heart, and tell it to me. And so did Targum Yonoson render, 'and their welfare you shall bring.'


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:18:4)


**עֲרֻבָּתָם.** לְשׁוֹן הַצָּלָה, כְּמוֹ ״עֲרֹב עַבְדְּךָ לְטוֹב״ (תהלים קיט:קכב), גרנטי״ש בלע״ז וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (שבת נו א): גֵּט כְּרִיתוּת יִקַּח מֵאִתָּם וְיָבִיא לִנְשׁוֹתֵיהֶם, לְהַפְרִיד עֵרוּב שֶׁבֵּינוֹ לְבֵינָהּ:


**Tidings of their welfare.** This is an expression of salvation, as in 'Assure your servant for good';¹¹*garantis* in O.F. Our Rabbis said [it means] that he should take a divorce from them, and deliver it to their wives, to break the connection [עֵרוּב] between him [husband] and her [wife].¹²

footnotes:
¹¹ Tehillim 119:122.
¹² It was customary for soldiers to divorce their wives upon leaving for war. This was done to spare the wives from any difficulty to remarry in case they did not return. See Maseches Kesubos 9b and Maseches Shabbos 56a.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:20:1)


**הַמַּעְגָּלָה.** סְבִיבוֹת הַמַּחֲנֶה עוֹשִׂים מַעְגָּל, וְהַיּוֹצֵא חוּץ, לְפָנִים אוֹ לְאָחוֹר, מִתְחַיֵּב. לְפָנִים, שֶׁמָּא יָרוּצוּ עָלָיו בְּנֵי מַעַרְכוֹת הָאוֹיְבִים, לְאָחוֹר, מְבַקֵּשׁ הוּא לָנוּס:


**To the barricade.** They would form a circle around the camp, and whoever would go out [of the circle], either in front of, or behind the camp, would be endangered; in front [of the circle] he might be attacked by the men of the enemy armies, and behind [the circle] because he [apparently] seeks to desert.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:21:1)


**וַתַּעֲרֹךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** מַעֲרֶכֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**Yisroel deployed.** Referring to Yisroel's battalions.¹³

footnotes:
¹³ That is why the feminine form [וַתַּעֲרֹךְ] is used because it is referring to מַעֲרָכָה which is grammatically feminine.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:22:1)


**שׁוֹמֵר הַכֵּלִים.** שֶׁל בְּנֵי הַצָּבָא:


**The keeper of the equipment.** Of the soldiers.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:23:1)


**מִמַּעֲרוֹת פְּלִשְׁתִּים.** כְּתִיב, רַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ (סוטה מב ב): מֵאָה בְּנֵי אָדָם הֶעֱרוּ בְּעָרְפָּה אִמּוֹ, כְּשֶׁפֵּרְשָׁה מֵאַחֲרֵי רוּת וְנָעֳמִי חֲמוֹתָהּ:


**From Philistines camp.** [The text] is written מִמַּעֲרוֹת; our Rabbis expounded [from the word מַעֲרוֹת]¹⁴ that one hundred men had relations with his [Golyas'] mother Orpoh, after she left Rus and her mother-in-law Naomi.¹⁵

footnotes:
¹⁴ The root being ערה and the noun ערוה. See Rashi Vayikra 20:18.
¹⁵ See Maseches Sotah 42b. Golyos was born from these relations and no one knew who fathered him so he became known as אִישׁ הַבֵּנַיִם ‘a man from between them.’ See above verse 4. Da’as Sofrim suggests that מְעָרוֹת [=caves] may have been dug out in the mountain the Philistines as a defensive measure in the case of an attack by the Bnei Yisroel.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:25:1)


**יַעֲשֶׂה חָפְשִׁי.** מִדְּבָרִים הָאֲמוּרִים בְּמִשְׁפְּטֵי הַמְּלוּכָה:


**He will exempt.** From the listed royal obligations.¹⁶

footnotes:
¹⁶ See above 8:11–17 for details of these obligations.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:34:1)


**וּבָא הָאֲרִי וְאֶת הַדֹּב.** (תרגום:) ״וַאֲתָא אַרְיָא וְאַף דֻּבָּא״:


**The lion came with a bear.** The lion and bear came [at the same time].



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:36:1)


**גַּם אֶת הָאֲרִי גַּם הַדֹּב.** הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ רִבּוּיִין, אֲרִי וּשְׁנֵי גוּרָיו, וְדֹב וּשְׁנֵי יְלָדָיו:


**Even the lion and the bear.** These words [גַּם, אֶת, גַּם] are of inclusive nature, meaning a lion and its two whelps, and a bear and its two cubs.¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁷ And Dovid slew all six.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:37:1)


**ה׳ אֲשֶׁר הִצִּילַנִי מִיַּד הָאֲרִי.** יוֹדֵעַ אֲנִי שֶׁלֹּא לְחִנָּם נִזְדַּמֵּן לִי הַדָּבָר הַהוּא, אֶלָּא שֶׁלֶּעָתִיד אֲנִי בָּא לִידֵי כַּיּוֹצֵא בָּהּ לִתְשׁוּעַת יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאֶסְמֹךְ עָלֶיהָ וְאֵצֵא וְזֶה אֶחָד מִשְּׁנֵי צַדִּיקִים שֶׁנָּתַן לָהֶם רֶמֶז, וְחָשׁוּ לְהַשְׂכִּיל לַדָּבָר, דָּוִד וּמָרְדֳּכַי, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (אסתר ב:יא): ״וּבְכָל יוֹם וָיוֹם מָרְדֳּכַי מִתְהַלֵּךְ לִפְנֵי חֲצַר וְגוֹ׳״, אָמַר: לֹא לְחִנָּם עָלְתָה בְּצַדֶּקֶת זוֹ שֶׁנִּתְפְּסָה בְּמִשְׁכַּב הֶעָרֵל הַזֶּה, אֶלָּא שֶׁעֲתִידָה הִיא לַעֲמוֹד לְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּיוֹם צָרָה:


**Adonoy Who saved me from the claws of the lion.** I know that this incident did not occur to me for naught, but as an indication that in the future, I would be confronted with similar circumstances, as a salvation for Yisroel. I will therefore rely on this, and go out. And he was one of two righteous men, to whom [God] gave a hint, and they understood the matter, Dovid and Mordechai, as it is stated: "And every day Mordechai would walk before the courtyard…"¹⁸ He said [to himself], 'It is not for naught that this righteous woman [Esther] has been taken into the bed of this uncircumcised one [Achashveirosh]. It can only mean that she is destined to stand up on behalf of Yisroel at a time of distress.'

footnotes:
¹⁸ Megillas Esther 2:11.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:38:1)


**אֶת דָּוִד מַדָּיו.** נֶהֶפְכוּ לִהְיוֹת כְּמִדַּת דָּוִד מִשֶּׁנִּמְשַׁח בְּשֶׁמֶן הַמִּשְׁחָה, אַף שֶׁהָיוּ שֶׁל שָׁאוּל שֶׁהָיָה גָּבוֹהַּ מִכָּל הָעָם מִשִּׁכְמוֹ וָמַעְלָה (שמואל א ט:ב), וְכֵיוָן שֶׁרָאָה שָׁאוּל כֵּן, הִכְנִיס בּוֹ עַיִן הָרַע, וְהִרְגִּישׁ דָּוִד בַּדָּבָר:


**Dovid in his own battle garments.** They changed and became Dovid's size,¹⁹ since he had been anointed with the anointing oil,²⁰ although they belonged to Shaul who was taller than all of the other people, from his shoulder and upward.²¹ And when Shaul noted this, he cast an envious glance toward him, and Dovid realized it.

footnotes:
¹⁹ The word מַדָיו is derived from מִדָה [=measure].
²⁰ See Maseches Yevamos 76b.
²¹ Above 9:2.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:39:1)


**וַיֹּאֶל לָלֶכֶת.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְלָא אָבָה לְמֵיזַל״. יֵשׁ תֵּיבוֹת מְשַׁמְּשׁוֹת לָשׁוֹן וְחִלּוּפוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״מְסָעֵף פֹּארָה״ (ישעיהו י:לג); ״וְשֵׁרֶשְׁךָ מֵאֶרֶץ חַיִּים״ (תהלים נב:ז):


**But he did not want to go out.** Targum Yonoson renders "and he did not want to go."²² There are words which have two opposite definitions, as in "will cut off [מְסָעֵף] a branch,"²³ [derived from סָעִיף, a branch]. [Also,] "and he will uproot you [וְשֵׁרֶשְׁךְ] from the land of the living," [derived from שׁוֹרֶש, a root].²⁴

footnotes:
²² Although וַיאֶׁל usually means ‘he wanted’ as in Shemos 2:21.
²³ Yeshayahu 10:33.
²⁴ Tehillim 52:7.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:39:2)


**כִּי לֹא נִסָּה.** (תרגום:) ״אֲרֵי לָא אַלִּיף״:


**For he was not accustomed.** I.e., because he was not accustomed.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:39:3)


**כִּי לֹא נִסִּיתִי.** (תרגום:) ״אֲרֵי לֵית בְּהוֹן נִסָּא״, אֵין שִׁבְחוֹ שֶׁל נֵס בְּכָךְ. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: וַיּוֹאֶל לָלֶכֶת, רָצָה לָלֶכֶת וְלִנְסוֹת, וְכַאֲשֶׁר רָאָה שֶׁהָיוּ כְּבֵדִים עָלָיו, הֱסִירָם:


**For I am not accustomed.** [Targum renders] 'for their is no miracle [נֵס] in them,' [meaning] there will be no praise for the miracle in this manner.²⁵ Another explanation of ,וַיאֶׁל לָלֶכֶת is, 'he wanted to go, and to try [wearing Shaul's battle garments],' but when he saw that they were too heavy for him, he took them off.

footnotes:
²⁵ Dovid wanted it to be clear to all that his victory was achieved solely by the intervention of ה׳. Another explanation why Dovid refused to wear Shaul’s battle garments was because he feared that by doing so would arouse Shaul’s jealousy thereby aggravating Shaul.—Da’as Sofrim



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:40:1)


**חַלֻּקֵי אֲבָנִים.** אֲבָנִים דַּקּוֹת וַחֲלָקוֹת:


**Smooth stones.** Thin and smooth stones.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:40:2)


**וּבַיַּלְקוּט.** (תרגום:) ״וּבְתַרְמִילֵיהּ״:


**And in his pouch.** [Targum Yonoson renders] 'and in his pack.'



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:44:1)


**וְלִבְהֶמַת הַשָּׂדֶה.** אֵין דֶּרֶךְ לִבְהֵמוֹת לֶאֱכֹל אָדָם, אָמַר דָּוִד: כְּבָר נִטְרְפָה דַּעְתּוֹ שֶׁל זֶה, שֶׁלִּי הוּא, בָּא דָוִד וְאָמַר לוֹ: **וְנָתַתִּי פֶּגֶר מַחֲנֵה פְלִשְׁתִּים וְגוֹ׳ וּלְחַיַּת הָאָרֶץ**:


**And to the cattle of the field.** It is not customary for cattle to eat human flesh. Dovid said [to himself], 'This one's mind is already deranged; He is mine.' Dovid [seeing this] said, 'and I will offer the carcasses of the Philistine camp …and to the beasts of the earth.'



### Verse 49

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:49:1)


**וַיִּפֹּל עַל פָּנָיו.** לֹא הָיָה לוֹ לִפּוֹל אֶלָּא לַאֲחוֹרָיו, שֶׁהֲרֵי עַל מִצְחוֹ הֻכָּה, אֶלָּא כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִצְטָעֵר דָּוִד לֵילֵךְ וְלַחְתֹּךְ אֶת רֹאשׁוֹ, שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּכֵּר דָּוִד עַכְשָׁיו שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה אַמּוֹת וְזִרְתַּיִם, שֶׁקּוֹמָתוֹ שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת וָזֶרֶת, נִשְׁתַּכֵּר מְלֹא קוֹמָה לַאֲחוֹרָיו, וּמְלֹא קוֹמָה לְפָנָיו (במדרש תהלים):


**And he fell face downward.** He should have fallen backwards since he had been struck on his forehead.²⁶ But, in order that Dovid should not be troubled to walk [the extra distance] and cut off his head, for Dovid now saved twelve *amos* and two *zeres;* for his height was six *amos* and a *zeres,* Dovid saved [walking] his entire height back and hs entire height forward.²⁷

footnotes:
²⁶ The impact should have made him fall backwards.
²⁷ Another reason why he fell face downward is because ה׳ had decreed that the mouth that blasphemed shall lick the dust of the earth.



### Verse 53

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:53:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:53:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:53:1)


**מִדְּלֹק.** מִרְדֹּף, כְּמוֹ ״דָּלַקְתָּ אַחֲרָי״ (בראשית לא:לו):


**From pursuing.** Meaning the same as מִרְדׂף [=pursuing] as in "you have pursued [דָלַקְתָּ] me."²⁸

footnotes:
²⁸ Bereishis 31:36



### Verse 55

###### Rashi on I Samuel 17:55:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:55:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/17:55:1)


**בֶּן מִי זֶה הַנַּעַר.** וְכִי לֹא הָיָה מַכִּירוֹ, וַהֲלֹא כְּתִיב (שמואל א טז:כא): ״וַיֶּאֱהָבֵהוּ מְאֹד וַיְהִי לוֹ נוֹשֵׂא כֵלִים״, אֶלָּא רָאָהוּ מִתְנַהֵג בְּטַכְסִיסֵי מַלְכוּת, אָמַר שָׁאוּל: אִם בָּא מִמִּשְׁפַּחַת פֶּרֶץ, מֶלֶךְ יִהְיֶה, שֶׁהַמֶּלֶךְ פּוֹרֵץ גָּדֵר לַעֲשׂוֹת לוֹ דֶּרֶךְ וְאֵין מְמַחִין בְּיָדוֹ, וְאִם מִמִּשְׁפַּחַת זֶרַח בָּא, חָשׁוּב יִהְיֶה. אָמַר לוֹ דּוֹאֵג: עַד שֶׁאַתָּה שׁוֹאֵל אִם בָּא מִמִּשְׁפָּחָה שֶׁיְּהֵא הוּא הָגוּן לַמַּלְכוּת אִם לָאו, שְׁאַל אִם רָאוּי לָבֹא בַּקָּהָל אִם לָאו, שֶׁהֲרֵי מֵרוּת הַמּוֹאֲבִיָּה בָּא. אָמַר לוֹ אַבְנֵר: שָׁנִינוּ עַמּוֹנִי וְלֹא עַמּוֹנִית. אָמַר לוֹ דּוֹאֵג: אִם כֵּן מַמְזֵר וְלֹא מַמְזֶרֶת. אָמַר לוֹ: כָּאן נֶאֱמַר (דברים כג:ה): ״עַל דְּבַר אֲשֶׁר לֹא קִדְּמוּ אֶתְכֶם בַּלֶּחֶם וּבַמַּיִם״, וְאֵין דֶּרֶךְ אִשָּׁה בְּכָךְ. אָמַר לוֹ: הָיָה לָהֶם לְקַדֵּם אֲנָשִׁים לִקְרַאת אֲנָשִׁים, וְנָשִׁים לִקְרַאת נָשִׁים. נִשְׁתַּתֵּק אַבְנֵר, אָמַר לוֹ שָׁאוּל: **שְׁאַל אַתָּה בֶּן מִי זֶה הָעֶלֶם.** לְמַעְלָה כְּתִיב ׳הַנַּעַר׳, וְכָאן כָּתוּב ׳הָעֶלֶם׳, אָמַר לוֹ: הֲלָכָה זוֹ נִתְעַלְּמָה מִמְּךָ, צֵא וּשְׁאַל בְּבֵית הַמִּדְרָשׁ, כִּדְאִיתָא בִּיבָמוֹת (עו ב):


**Whose son is this youth.** Our Rabbis said, "Did he not recognize him? Is it not stated, "He loved him very much and he became his arms bearer"?²⁹ But, he saw him behaving in a kingly manner. Shaul said, 'If he comes from the family of Peretz, he will become king, for the king may break fences to make way for himself, and no one may object. If, however, he comes from the family of Zerach, he will become an important person. Do'eg said to him, 'Before you ask whether or not he is descended from a family which qualifies him for the throne, ask whether or not he is qualified to enter [by marriage] into the congregation [of Yisroel], because he is descended from Rus the Mo'avite.' Avneir responded to him, 'We have learned that an Ammonite [is forbidden]³⁰ but not a female Ammonite [the same holding true for a female Moavite].' So Doeg said to him, 'If so, only a male *momzer* and not a *momzeres* [=female *momzer*] should be foridden.' He replied, 'It is stated here, "because they did not meet you with bread and water,³¹ and it is not customary for a woman to do so."' He said to him, 'Men should have met men, and women should have met women [with bread and water].' Avneir became silent. Then Shaul said to him": **Ask whose son this young man is.** Above it is written 'הַנַּעַר'³² and here it is written 'הָעָלֶם'. He said to him, "This law has escaped your memory [נִתְעַלְּמָה]. Go out and ask in the house of study," as it is stated in [Maseches] Yevomos.³³

footnotes:
²⁹ Above 16:21. Ralbag suggests that Dovid would now marry his daughter and Shaul therefore inquired about his genealogy.
³⁰ Devarim 23:4.
³¹ Ibid., verse 5.
³² Verse 55.



## Chapter 18


### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:6:1)


**וּבְשָׁלִשִׁים.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּבְצִלְצְלִין״. מִין כְּלֵי זֶמֶר:


**And cymbals.** Targum Yonoson renders, 'and with cymbals,' a type of musical instrument.¹

footnotes:
¹ Perhaps a three [=שָׁלשׁ] stringed harp.—Radak.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:8:1)


**וְעוֹד לוֹ.** וְעוֹד לוֹ מַה הוּא נֶחְסָר שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְהוֹסִיף לוֹ:


**He is lacking.** What more is he lacking, that he needs to acquire?


###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:8:2)


**אַךְ הַמְּלוּכָה.** אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ עוֹד אֶלָּא לַמַּלְכוּת:


**Only the kingship.** He needs nothing else but the kingship.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:9:1)


**עוֹיֵן.** (תרגום:) ״עַיִן רָעָה״:


**Viewed suspiciously.** With an envious glance.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:10:1)


**יִתְנַבֵּא.** ״וְאִשְׁתַּטֵּי״ נָבִיא וְשׁוֹטֶה מְדַבְּרִים דִּבְרֵי רְמָזִים שֶׁאֵינָם נִכָּרִים:


**And he raved.** [Literally 'he prophesied'] but Targum renders, 'and he raved.' A prophet and a madman speak in unintelligible hints.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:11:1)


**אַכֶּה בְדָוִד וּבַקִּיר.** מַכָּה אַחַת אַכֶּה, שֶׁיִּכָּנֵס הַחֲנִית בְּדָוִד וּבַקִּיר:


**I will strike Dovid and the wall.** I will strike one blow so, that the spear will pierce Dovid and the wall.²

footnotes:
² Shaul wanted to strike Dovid in a manner that it would appear that it was unintentional.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:14:1)


**מַשְׂכִּיל.** מַצְלִיחַ:


**Succeeded.** Succeeded.³

footnotes:
³ Although ‘שֵׂבֶל’ usually indicates wisdom, here it is used to indicate success.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:17:1)


**וְשָׁאוּל אָמַר.** בְּלִבּוֹ:


**Shaul said.** To himself.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:17:2)


**אַל תְּהִי יָדִי בוֹ.** פֶּן אֶעָנֵשׁ:


**Let not my hand be upon him.** Lest I will be punished.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:17:3)


**וּתְהִי בוֹ יַד פְּלִשְׁתִּים.** לְכָךְ אָמַר לוֹ וְהִלָּחֵם מִלְחֲמוֹת ה׳:


**But let the hand of the Philistines be upon him.** Therefore, he said to him, "and fight Adonoy's wars."



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:19:1)


**בְּעֵת תֵּת.** כְּשֶׁהִגִּיעַ זְמַן שֶׁקָּבְעוּ לְתִתָּהּ לוֹ, וּבְעוֹד שֶׁהָיוּ מִתְעַסְּקִים לְתִתָּהּ לוֹ, נִתְגַּלְגֵּל הַדָּבָר וְנִתְּנָה לְעַדְרִיאֵל:


**When the time came to give.** When the time arrived which they had set to give her to him, and while they were involved with giving her to him, it turned out that she was given to Adrieil.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Son of Barzili. See II Shmuel 21:8. She married Adrieil without Shaul’s knowledge—Metzudas Dovid.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:21:1)


**בִּשְׁתַּיִם תִּתְחַתֵּן בִּי.** שְׁתֵּי בָּנוֹת יֵשׁ לִי, בְּאַחַת מֵהֶן תִּתְחַתֵּן בִּי וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״בַּחֲדָא מִתְּרֵין״. כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ (שמואל ב כד:יב): שָׁלֹשׁ אָנֹכִי נוֹטֵל עָלֶיךָ. אַחַת מִשָּׁלֹשׁ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ שָׁם: בְּחַר לְךָ אַחַת מֵהֶם:


**Through the second [of my two daughters] you will become my son-in-law.** I have two daughters; through one of them, you will become my son-in-law.⁵ And similarly did Targum Yonoson render, 'with one of the two.' Similarly [we find], "I hold three things [meaning, one of three things] over you,"⁶ as Scripture clarifies there, "choose one of them."⁷

footnotes:
⁵ See Maseches Sanhedrin 19b.
⁶ II Shmuel 24:12.
⁷ Ibid.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:26:1)


**וְלֹא מָלְאוּ הַיָּמִים.** לֹא הִמְתִּין עַד מְלֹאת הַיָּמִים שֶׁקָּבַע לוֹ לְהָבִיא אֶת מֵאָה הָעֲרָלוֹת:


**Time had not yet expired.** He did not wait until the time expired which he had set for him to bring the one hundred foreskins.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:27:1)


**וַיָּקָם דָּוִד וַיֵּלֶךְ הוּא וַאֲנָשָׁיו וְגוֹ׳ וַיְמַלְאוּם.** פרישנטי״ר בלע״ז:


**Dovid rose and went, he and his men…and gave them all.⁸** *Presenter* in O.F.

footnotes:
⁸ I.e., all two hundred, though Shaul demanded only one hundred.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:29:1)


**לְרֹא.** לִירְאָה:


**To fear.** To fear.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on I Samuel 18:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/18:30:1)


**וַיֵּצְאוּ שָׂרֵי פְלִשְׁתִּים.** לָבֹא בִּגְדוּדִים לִשְׁלֹל שָׁלָל בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


**The Philistines officers would venture out.** To come in troops to plunder [the Bnei] Yisroel.



## Chapter 19


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:3:1)


**לְיַד אָבִי.** סָמוּךְ לְאָבִי, איינשיי״ש בלע״ז:


**Next to my father.** Near my father; *en ses aises* in O.F.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:13:1)


**וַתִּקַּח מִיכַל.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יָבִינוּ שֶׁבָּרַח וְיִרְדְּפוּ אַחֲרָיו:


**Michal took.** So that they would not realize that he fled, and pursue him.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:13:2)


**אֶת הַתְּרָפִים.** עֲשׂוּיִים בִּדְמוּת גּוּף אָדָם:


**A manlike figure.** Made in the likeness of a human body.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:13:3)


**כְּבִיר הָעִזִּים.** עוֹר מֻפְשָׁט שָׁלֵם כְּחֵמֶת, פלוצי״ל בלע״ז:


**A goatskin.** A hide skinned in one piece, like a skin bottle. *Flocele* in O.F.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:13:4)


**שָׂמָה מְרַאֲשֹׁתָיו.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין לַתְּרָפִים שֵׂעָר, וְזֶה דּוֹמֶה לִשְׂעַר אָדָם:


**She put…at its head.** Because the manlike figure has no hair, and this resembles human hair.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:17:1)


**הוּא אָמַר אֵלַי וְגוֹ׳.** אַתָּה מְסַרְתַּנִי בְּיַד לִסְטִים אֶחָד, שָׁלַף חַרְבּוֹ עָלַי עַד שֶׁשִּׁלַּחְתִּיו:


**He said to me…** You delivered me into the hands of a bandit.¹ He drew his sword at me until I let him leave.

footnotes:
¹ She placed the blame for Dovid’s escape on Shaul.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:19:1)


**בְּנָיוֹת.** (תרגום:) ״בְּבֵית אוּלְפָנָא״:


**In Nayos.** In the house of study.²

footnotes:
² Radak explains that נָיוֹת is derived from the word נָוֶה [=beauty], referring to the Beis Hamikdosh, which is the beauty of the world. They were sitting in Romoh engaged and seeking a location for the Beis Hamikdosh, the beauty of the world.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:20:1)


**לַהֲקַת.** (תרגום:) ״סִיעַת״:


**A group of.** A company.³

footnotes:
³ The letters לַהֲקַת are transposed from קהלת [=a group or company]; קָהָל includes the entire group and חֶבֶל [Above 10:5] means only part of the group—Malbim.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:24:1)


**וַיִּפְשַׁט גַּם הוּא בְּגָדָיו.** בִּגְדֵי מַלְכוּת, לִלְבּוֹשׁ בִּגְדֵי הַתַּלְמִידִים:


**He too removed his garments.** His royal garments, to put on his students' garments.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 19:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/19:24:2)


**וַיִּפֹּל עָרֹם.** וּנְפַל בִּרְשַׁן, תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּנְפַל עַרְטִילַאי״. וּבְשֵׁם רַבִּי מְנַחֵם שֶׁשָּׁמַע מִפִּי עֲרָבִי אֶחָד: ׳בִּרְשַׁן׳ בִּלְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי מְשֻׁגָּע:


**He fell down stripped.** Targum Yonoson renders וּנְפַל בִּרְשַׁן 'and he fell naked.' And in the name of R. Menachem, who heard from an Arab, בִּרְשָׁן in Arabic [means] insane.



## Chapter 20


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Samuel 20:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/20:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/20:3:1)


**כְּפֶשַׁע.** כִּפְסִיעָה אַחַת נִפְטַרְתִּי מִפָּנָיו, וְהִכָּה בַּחֲנִית בַּקִּיר, בְּאוֹתָהּ פְּסִיעָה נִצַּלְתִּי מִן הַמָּוֶת:


**There is but a step.** With one step I left from being before him,¹ and he drove the spear into the wall. With that step, I was saved from death.

footnotes:
¹ Rashi’s interpretation follows the literal meaning. Others interpret this phrase figuratively, i.e., there is but a short distance between me and death—Radak.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Samuel 20:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/20:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/20:5:1)


**הִנֵּה חֹדֶשׁ מָחָר.** חִדּוּשׁ הַלְּבָנָה, וְכָל אוֹכְלֵי שֻׁלְחַן הַמֶּלֶךְ אֵין נִמְנָע אִישׁ מִלָּבֹא בְּיוֹם טוֹב אֶל הַלֶּחֶם:


**Behold, tomorrow is the New Moon.** The renewal of the moon.² And of all those who eat at the king's table, no one declines from coming to the repast on the festive day.³

footnotes:
² From the word חָדָשׁ [=new]. He could not state that tomorrow is Rosh Chodesh, because Rosh Chodesh could only be declared by בֵּית דִין and the actual day of Rosh Chodesh is unknown until it is sanctified by בֵּית דִין.
