**כַּעֲשֶׂרֶת הַיָּמִים.** שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי אֶבְלוֹ שֶׁל שְׁמוּאֵל תָּלָה לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁלֹּא יִתְעָרֵב אֵבֶל רָשָׁע בְּאֶבְלוֹ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ חָלָה שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים וָמֵת, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וַיִּגֹּף ה׳ אֶת נָבָל״, וְאָמַר מַר (שמחות ג ט): חָלָה שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים וָמֵת, זוֹ הִיא מִיתַת מַגֵּפָה. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (ראש השנה יח א): אֵלּוּ עֲשֶׂרֶת יְמֵי תְשׁוּבָה, שֶׁהִמְתִּין לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁיַּחֲזוֹר בִּתְשׁוּבָה. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: כְּנֶגֶד עֶשֶׂר לְגִימוֹת שֶׁנָּתַן נָבָל לְעַבְדֵי דָוִד, שֶׁשָּׁלַח לוֹ דָּוִד עֲשָׂרָה נְעָרִים וְהֶאֱכִילָן:


**After ten days.** There were seven days of mourning for Shmuel, so the Holy One, Blessed is He, suspended his sentence so that it should not coincide the mourning for this wicked man with his mourning period. Afterward, he became ill for three days and died, as it is stated, "And Adonoy inflicted Novol," and a Talmudist had stated that if one was ill three days and died, it is a death of a plague. However, our Rabbis stated that these are the Ten Days of Repentance which the Holy One, Blessed is He, waited for him to repent. But, some say that they correspond to the ten meals²⁵ which Novol gave Dovid's servants, for Dovid had sent him ten youths, and he fed them.

footnotes:
²⁵ See above verses 5,9, and Maharsha in Rosh Hashanah 18a.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on I Samuel 25:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:39:1)


**אֶת רִיב חֶרְפָּתִי.** גִּדּוּפַי שֶׁחֵרְפַנִי (שמואל א פסוק י): הַיּוֹם רַבּוּ עֲבָדִים הַמִּתְפָּרְצִים:


**The battle of my disgrace.** My insult which he insulted me today [saying], 'Nowadays there are many servants who break away.'²⁶

footnotes:
²⁶ Above verse 10.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 25:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/25:39:2)


**וְאֶת עַבְדּוֹ חָשַׂךְ מֵרָעָה.** שֶׁלֹּא הֲרַגְתִּיו:


**And has spared His servant from evil.** That I did not kill him.



## Chapter 26


### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:7:1)


**מְעוּכָה.** נְעוּצָה:


**Stuck.** Thrust.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:10:1)


**חַי ה׳.** יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים, לְיִצְרוֹ נִשְׁבַּע, וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים, לַאֲבִישַׁי נִשְׁבַּע, אִם תַּהֲרֹג אֶת הַצַּדִּיק, אֲנִי אֲעָרֵב דָּמְךָ בְּדָמוֹ:


**As Adonoy lives.** Some say that he swore to his temptation;¹ others say that he swore to Avishai, that if you kill this righteous man [Shaul], I shall mingle your blood with his blood.

footnotes:
¹ The use of swearing to ward off temptation is done by many Tzadikim. For example, Avrohom swore [Bereishis 14:22-23] that he will not take the spoils of victory after defeating the four kings. It was Dovid’s inclination to kill Shaul for trying to kill him. He therefore swore that he will not kill Shaul.—See Midrash Tanchuma Beha’aloscha 10.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:10:2)


**כִּי אִם ה׳ יִגָּפֶנּוּ.** יְקָרֵב מִיתָתוֹ:


**Adonoy will strike him.** Will bring his death prematurely.²

footnotes:
² Like Novol.—Radak.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:10:3)


**אוֹ יוֹמוֹ יָבֹא.** יוֹם שֶׁזְּמַן מִיתָתוֹ בּוֹ:


**Or his day will come.** The day in which he is destined to die.³

footnotes:
³ Is in the very near future.—Radak.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:11:1)


**צַפַּחַת.** צְלוֹחִית:


**Flask.** Jug.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19:1)


**הֱסִיתְךָ בִי.** לִשְׂנָאָתִי, כָּל הֲסָתָה לְשׁוֹן שׂוּמָה, אמיטמנ״ט בלע״ז:


**Who has incited you against me.** To hate me. Every expression of הֲסָתָה means setting;⁴*ametement* in O.F.

footnotes:
⁴ See Devarim 13:7, where Rashi states ‘הַסָּתָה always denotes inciting.’


###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19:2)


**יָרַח מִנְחָה.** יְקַבֵּל בְּרַחֲמִים תְּפִלָּתִי, לְהָשִׁיב חֲמָתְךָ מִמֶּנִּי:


**Let my offering be pleasing to Him.** He will accept with mercy my prayer to turn your anger away from me.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19:3)


**כִּי גֵרְשׁוּנִי.** מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**For they have driven me away.** From Eretz Yisroel.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:19:4)


**לֵךְ עֲבֹד אֱלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים.** הַיּוֹצֵא מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְחוּץ לָאָרֶץ בִּזְמַן הַבַּיִת, כְּאִלּוּ עוֹבֵד עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״אִיזִיל דָּוִד בֵּינֵי עַמְמַיָּא פָּלְחֵי טָעוּתָא״:


**Go serve other gods.** Whoever goes out of Eretz Yisroel to other lands during the time of the Temple is considered as though he worshipped idols.⁵ Targum Yonoson renders, 'Go, Dovid, among nations who worship idols.'⁶

footnotes:
⁵ See Maseches Kesubos 110b.
⁶ Or the reference to idol worship means that by causing Dovid to flee from his enemies made him abandon his Torah study thereby moving him closer to idolatry.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:20:1)


**הַקֹּרֵא.** עוֹף שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ קוֹרֵא, ובלע״ז פרדי״ץ, וְכֵן (ירמיהו יז:יא): ״קֹרֵא דָגַר וְלֹא יָלָד״, וּמְרַדֵּף אַחַר קִנֵּי שְׁאָר עוֹפוֹת, וְיוֹשֵׁב עַל בֵּיצֵיהֶם:


**As the partridge.** A bird named קוֹרֵא, and in O.F. *perdriz;* and similarly, "as the partridge calls together [offspring] it did not bear"⁷ and it seeks the nests of other birds, and sits on their eggs.⁸

footnotes:
⁷ Yirmiyahu 17:11.
⁸ Maseches Chulin 140b. Dovid compared Shaul’s fate to that of the partridge. Just as the partridge seeks the nests of other birds but the young return to their natural mother and the partridge is unable to keep them, so too, Shaul who chased after Dovid will be unable to keep his kingship.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on I Samuel 26:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/26:23:1)


**יָשִׁיב לָאִישׁ.** לְכָל צַדִּיק וְצַדִּיק:


**Each man.** To each righteous man.⁹

footnotes:
⁹ Who does as I have done.—Radak. Dovid was hinting to ה׳ that just as Shaul’s life was precious in his eyes, so too may his life be precious in the eyes of ה׳.



## Chapter 27


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Samuel 27:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:1:1)


**עַתָּה אֶסָּפֶה יוֹם אֶחָד.** שֶׁמָּא יֶאֱרֹב וְיָבֹא עָלַי פִּתְאוֹם:


**Now I might perish one day.** Perhaps he will ambush me and suddenly attack me.¹

footnotes:
¹ Dovid did not trust Shaul and was convinced that he was laying a trap for him by asking him to remain.—Metzudas Dovid



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on I Samuel 27:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:5:1)


**וְלָמָּה יֵשֵׁב עַבְדְּךָ וְגוֹ׳.** וְאֶהְיֶה לְךָ לְמַשָּׂא:


**Why should your servant remain…** And be a burden to you.²

footnotes:
² Probably, Dovid did not want to live with idolaters and therefore asked in a diplomatic fashion, e.g., ‘Why should your servant be a burden to you,’ to settle in a separate city.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on I Samuel 27:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:7:1)


**יָמִים וְאַרְבָּעָה חֳדָשִׁים.** מִעוּט יָמִים שְׁנַיִם, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְפָרֵשׁ יָמִים זֶה שָׁנָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא הָלַךְ דָּוִד אֵצֶל אָכִישׁ עַד שֶׁמֵּת שְׁמוּאֵל, וְשָׁאוּל לֹא מָלַךְ אַחַר מִיתַת שְׁמוּאֵל אֶלָּא אַרְבָּעָה חֳדָשִׁים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם וְגַם מִמִּנְיַן שְׁנוֹתָיו שֶׁל עֵלִי, וְעֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה שֶׁהָיָה הָאָרוֹן בְּקִרְיַת יְעָרִים, הֲרֵי שִׁשִּׁים שָׁנָה, צֵא מֵהֶם חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם שֶׁל שְׁמוּאֵל, וְשָׁנָה אַחַת לְעִבּוּרוֹ עִם אַרְבָּעָה חֳדָשִׁים הַלָּלוּ, וְשֶׁבַע שָׁנִים שֶׁמָּלַךְ דָּוִד בְּחֶבְרוֹן קֹדֶם שֶׁהֵבִיא אֶת הָאָרוֹן מִקִּרְיַת יְעָרִים, נִמְצָא שֶׁלֹּא מָלַךְ שָׁאוּל אַחַר מִיתַת שְׁמוּאֵל, יוֹתֵר מֵאַרְבָּעָה חֳדָשִׁים:


**Days and four months.** The minimum [number] of days is two. It is impossible to explain this word, 'יָמִים' as a year³ because Dovid did not go to Achish until Shmuel had died, and Shaul only reigned after Shmuel's death⁴ four months, as we learned in Seder Olam. Also [this can be deduced from the following:] the number of Eili's years [forty years which he judged Yisroel],⁵ and the twenty years that the Ark was in Kiryas Ye'arim⁶ total sixty years. Subtract from that Shmuel's fifty-two years, plus one year from his conception, with these four months, plus the seven years which Dovid reigned in Chevron⁷ before he brought the Ark from Kiryas Ye'arim. This [calculation] discloses that Shaul reigned after Shmuel's death but four months.

footnotes:
³ As we find יָמִים to mean ‘year’ in Bereishis 41:1.
⁴ Therefore יָמִים is to be taken literally, i.e., days.
⁵ Above 4:18.
⁶ Above 7:2.
⁷ II Shmuel 2:5.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Samuel 27:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:8:1)


**וַיַּעַל דָּוִד.** לְשׁוֹן הוֹוֶה, הוּא תָּמִיד הָיָה רָגִיל בְּכָךְ:


**Dovid…would go up.** This is the present tense, i.e., he was always accustomed doing this.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 27:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:8:2)


**בּוֹאֲךָ שׁוּרָה.** (תרגום:) ״מִן מַעֲלָנָא דְחַגְרָא״:


**In the direction of Shur.** From the entrance of Shur.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Samuel 27:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/27:10:1)


**אֶל פְּשַׁטְתֶּם.** לָמֶ״ד מִתְחַלֶּפֶת בְּנוּ״ן, כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּעַשׂ לוֹ נִשְׁכָּה״ בְּסֵפֶר עֶזְרָא (נחמיה יג:ז), כְּמוֹ לִשְׁכָּה:


**Where did you raid.** The 'ל' is interchangeable with a 'נ' [so the word אֶל=אָן which means 'where'], as in "he prepared a large chamber [נִשְׁכָּה],"⁸ in the Book of Ezra, meaning the equivalent of לִשְׁכָּה [=chamber].

footnotes:
⁸ Nechemyoh 13:7.



## Chapter 28


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:3:1)


**וּשְׁמוּאֵל מֵת.** הֲרֵי כְּבָר נֶאֱמַר ״וַיָּמָת שְׁמוּאֵל״, אֶלָּא לְפִי שֶׁבָּא לְדַבֵּר בְּשָׁאוּל שֶׁהוּצְרַךְ לִדְרֹשׁ בְּבַעֲלַת אוֹב, פָּתַח וְאָמַר ״וּשְׁמוּאֵל מֵת״, שֶׁאִלּוּ הָיָה קַיָּם, מִמֶּנּוּ הָיָה שָׁאוּל דּוֹרֵשׁ, וְשָׁאוּל הֵסִיר הָאוֹבוֹת, וְהֻצְרַךְ לְבַקֵּשׁ אִשָּׁה בַּעֲלַת אוֹב:


**Shmuel had died.** It was already stated, "and Shmuel died,"¹ but since [Scripture] came to speak about Shaul, that he had to ask of a medium of אוֹב,², it begins by stating, "and Shmuel had died," for had he been alive, Shaul would have inquired of him. But since Shaul had abolished the אוֹב mediums,³ he had to seek out find an אוֹב medium.

footnotes:
¹ Above 25:1.
² See Maseches Sanhedrin 65b for description of אוֹב.
³ Pursuant to the prohibition stated in Vayikra 19:31. Shaul removed the mediums after Shmuel died. Before that, it was not necessary for him to do so because while Shmuel was alive, prophecy was widespread in Yisroel, so no one sought out the medium of אוֹב or יׅדְּעֹנִי.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:3:2)


**וַיִּסְפְּדוּ לוֹ כָל יִשְׂרָאֵל וַיִּקְבְּרֻהוּ בָרָמָה וּבְעִירוֹ.** (תרגום:) וּסְפָדוּ עֲלוֹהִי כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וּקְבָרוּהִי בְּרָמָתָא וּסְפָדוּ עֲלוֹהִי אֱנָשׁ בְּקַרְתֵּיהּ״; ׳וּבְעִירוֹ׳ מוּסָב עַל ׳וַיִּסְפְּדוּ לוֹ׳, סְפָדוּהוּ בָרָמָה כְּשֶׁקְּבָרוּהוּ, וּסְפָדוּהוּ אִישׁ בְּעִירוֹ״:


**And all of Yisroel mourned him and buried him in Romoh, in his city.** And all Yisroel mourned him and buried him in Romoh, and each person mourned him in his own city; and 'in his city' is connected with 'and they mourned him,' i.e., they mourned and buried him in Romoh, and each person mourned him in his own city.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ In the same manner as he was mourned in his home town of Romoh.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:6:1)


**גַּם בָּאוּרִים.** לְפִי שֶׁהָרַג נוֹב עִיר הַכֹּהֲנִים, לֹא נַעֲנָה:


**Nor through the Urim.** Because he had slain [the kohanim of] Nov,⁵ the City of Kohanim, he was not answered.

footnotes:
⁵ See above Chapter 22.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:8:1)


**וַיִּתְחַפֵּשׂ שָׁאוּל.** ״שִׁנָּה בְּגָדָיו, וְכֵן ״הִתְחַפֵּשׂ וָבֹא בַמִּלְחָמָה״ (מלכים א כב:ל); וְכֵן ״בְּרָב כֹּחַ יִתְחַפֵּשׂ לְבוּשִׁי״ (איוב ל:יח), וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְאִשְׁתַּנִּי שָׁאוּל:


**Shaul disguised himself.** He changed his clothes, and similarly, 'disguise yourself [הִתְחַפֵּשׂ] and come into battle,'⁶ and similarly, 'its intensity makes me change [יׅתְחַפֵּשׂ] my clothing.'⁷ And so did Targum Yonoson render, 'and Shaul changed.'

footnotes:
⁶ II Melochim 22:30.
⁷ Iyov 30:18.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:8:2)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ אֶל הָאִשָּׁה לָיְלָה.** יוֹם הָיָה, אֶלָּא מִתּוֹךְ צָרָתָם הָיָה דּוֹמֶה לָהֶם לַיְלָה, כָּךְ דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (פרשת אמור ב):


**They came to the woman at night.** [They actually came] during the daytime, but because of their distress, it seemed to them as night. So did R. Tanchuma expound.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:9:1)


**מִתְנַקֵּשׁ.** מְבַקֵּשׁ תַּקָּלָה:


**To entrap.** Seek a stumbling block [i.e., trap].



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:12:1)


**וַתִּזְעַק בְּקוֹל גָּדוֹל.** שֶׁרָאֲתָהוּ עוֹלֶה שֶׁלֹּא כְּדֶרֶךְ הָעוֹלִים, שֶׁהַמַּעֲלֶה בִּזְכוּרוֹ, עוֹלִים רַגְלֵיהֶם לְמַעְלָה, וְזֶה עוֹלֶה רֹאשׁוֹ לְמַעְלָה, בִּשְׁבִיל כְּבוֹדוֹ שֶׁל שָׁאוּל (תנחומא שם):


**She screamed loudly.** For she saw him ascending not in the usual manner⁸ of those who ascend [through the אוֹב medium], for when one conjures up [the dead], they ascend with their feet upward, while this one was ascending with his head upward, in honor of Shaul.⁹

footnotes:
⁸ Radak suggests that the moment the woman saw the manner in which Shmuel came up, she realized that the man who had come to her was Shaul, and that is why she screamed.
⁹ Vayikra Rabboh 26:7.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:13:1)


**אֱלֹהִים רָאִיתִי עֹלִים מִן הָאָרֶץ.** מַלְאָכִים שְׁנַיִם, מֹשֶׁה וּשְׁמוּאֵל, שֶׁנִּתְיָרֵא שְׁמוּאֵל שֶׁמָּא אֲנִי מִתְבַּקֵּשׁ בַּדִּין, וְהֶעֱלָה מֹשֶׁה עִמּוֹ (כדאיתא בחגיגה ד ב):


**I saw a godly man rising from the earth.** Two angels, Moshe and Shmuel,¹⁰ for Shmuel feared, 'Perhaps I am being summoned for judgement,' and he therefore brought Moshe up with him,¹¹ as it is stated in [Maseches] Chagiga and Ta'anis.¹²

footnotes:
¹⁰ This is alluded to in the words אִישׁ זֲקִן עֹלֶה וְהוּא עֹטֶה מְעִיל, ‘an old man ascends [i.e., Moshe] and he is wearing a robe [i.e., Shmuel].’
¹¹ Each person must face judgment within twelve months of his death. Shmuel wanted that Moshe testify on his behalf.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:14:1)


**מַה תָּאֳרוֹ.** שְׁלֹשָׁה דְבָרִים נֶאֶמְרוּ בָּאוֹב: הַמַּעֲלֶה אוֹתוֹ, רוֹאֵהוּ וְאֵינוֹ שׁוֹמֵעַ קוֹלוֹ, וְהַנִּשְׁאָל לוֹ, שׁוֹמְעוֹ וְאֵינוֹ רוֹאֵהוּ, וּלְפִיכָךְ שָׁאַל מַה תָּאֳרוֹ, וְאַחֵר, לֹא רוֹאֵהוּ וְלֹא שׁוֹמְעוֹ (תנחומא שם):


**What does he look like.** Three things were said regarding אוֹב: The one who conjures him [the dead] up, sees him, but does not hear his voice; the one who inquires of him, hears him, but does not see him, and therefore, he [Shaul] asked, 'What does he look like?', and the others neither see or hear him.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:14:2)


**וְהוּא עֹטֶה מְעִיל.** שֶׁהָיָה רָגִיל לִלְבֹּשׁ מְעִיל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּמְעִיל קָטֹן תַּעֲשֶׂה לּוֹ אִמּוֹ״ (שמואל א ב:יט), וּבִמְעִילוֹ נִקְבַּר, וְכֵן עָלָה, וְכֵן לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא יַעַמְדוּ בִּלְבוּשֵׁיהֶם (תנחומא שם):


**He is garbed in a robe.** For he was accustomed to wear a robe, as it is stated, "and his mother would make him a small robe."¹³ He was buried with his robe, and so he arose. And so in the future [Resurrection of the Dead], they will rise with their garments.

footnotes:
¹³ Above 2:19.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:15:1)


**הִרְגַּזְתָּנִי.** הֶחֱרַדְתָּנִי:


**Have you disturbed me.** You have frightened me.¹⁴

footnotes:
¹⁴ The Midrash explains that Shmuel feared that his day of judgment had come. See above verse—Radak


###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:15:2)


**גַּם בְּיַד הַנְּבִיאִים גַּם בַּחֲלֹמוֹת.** אֲבָל בָּאוּרִים וְתֻמִּים לֹא אָמַר לוֹ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשָּׁאַל בָּהֶם כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה, שֶׁהָיָה בּוֹשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ עַל שֶׁהָרַג נוֹב עִיר הַכֹּהֲנִים:


**Neither through the prophets nor through dreams.** But he did not mention to him the Urim and Tumim, even though he had inquired of them, as it is stated above,¹⁵ because he was ashamed of it, since he had slain [the kohanim of] Nov, the City of the Kohanim.¹⁶

footnotes:
¹⁵ Verse 6.
¹⁶ Maseches Berachos 12b and Rashi there.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:15:3)


**וָאֶקְרָאֶה לְךָ.** לְשׁוֹן וָאֶזְעַק אַחֲרֶיךָ, כְּמוֹ ״נִקְרֹא נִקְרֵיתִי בְּהַר הַגִּלְבֹּעַ״ (שמואל ב א:ו); וּכְמוֹ ״וְשָׁם נִקְרָא אִישׁ בְּלִיַּעַל״ (שמואל ב כ:א), הֻצְרַכְתִּי לִהְיוֹת נִקְרָא אֵלֶיךָ, שֶׁתּוֹדִיעֵנִי מָה אֶעֱשֶׂה:


**Therefore I called upon you.** An expression meaning, 'and I have been summoned after you,' as in, "I was summoned [נִקְראׁ נִקְרֵיתִי] on Mount Gilbo'a,"¹⁷ and as in "and there chanced [נִקְרָא] an unscrupulous man."¹⁸ It was necessary for me to be summoned after you, that you let me know what I should do.

footnotes:
¹⁷ II Shmuel 1:6.
¹⁸ Ibid. 20:1.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:16:1)


**וְלָמָּה תִּשְׁאָלֵנִי.** הֲרֵי שָׁאַלְתָּ מִן הַנְּבִיאִים הַחַיִּים:


**But why do you ask me.** Since you have inquired of the living prophets.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:16:2)


**וַיְהִי עָרֶךָ.** (תרגום:) ״וַהֲוָה בְּסַעֲדֵיהּ דְּגַבְרָא דְאַתְּ בְּעֵיל דּבָבֵיהּ״:


**And become your adversary.** He has become the supporter of the one whose adversary you are.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ I.e., Dovid.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:16:3)


**עָרֶךָ.** לְשׁוֹן שׂוֹנֵא וְכַמָּה יֵשׁ בַּמִּקְרָא, ״וּמָלְאוּ פְנֵי תֵבֵל עָרִים״ (ישעיהו יד:כא); ״וְהִשְׁמַדְתִּי עָרֶיךָ״ (מיכה ה:יג); ״חֶלְמָא לְשָׂנְאָךְ וּפִשְׁרֵהּ לְעָרָךְ״ (דניאל ד:טז):


**Adversary.** An expression meaning an enemy. In Scripture there are many [examples of the word עׇרֶךָ], "and the world will become full with enemies [עׇרִים]";²⁰ "and I will destroy your enemies [עׇרֶיךָ]";²¹ "may this dream be upon your enemies and its interpretation upon your adversaries [לְעָרָךְ]";²²

footnotes:
²⁰ Yeshay 14:21.
²¹ Micha 5:13.
²² Daniel 4:16.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:17:1)


**לְרֵעֲךָ לְדָוִד.** וּבְחַיָּיו לֹא הִזְכִּיר לוֹ שְׁמוֹ, אֶלָּא ״וּנְתָנָהּ לְרֵעֲךָ הַטּוֹב מִמְּךָ״ (שמואל א טו:כח), לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה יָרֵא מִמֶּנּוּ שֶׁלֹּא יַהַרְגֵנּוּ, לְפִי שֶׁמְּשָׁחוֹ לַמַּלְכוּת (תנחומא שם):


**To your fellow, to Dovid.** But during his lifetime, he [Shmuel] did not mention his name [Dovid] to him, only 'and He gave it to your fellow who is better than you'²³ because he feared that he [Shaul], might kill him,²⁴ because he had anointed him [Dovid] as king.

footnotes:
²³ Above 15:28.
²⁴ Now however, that Shmuel was in the עוֹלָם הָאֶמֶת [=World of Truth] there was nothing to fear speaking the complete truth.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:19:1)


**אַתָּה וּבָנֶיךָ עִמִּי.** בִּמְחִצָּתִי:


**You and your sons will be with me.** In my abode.²⁵

footnotes:
²⁵ See Maseches Berachos 12b. By saying this to Shaul, Shmuel indicated to Shaul that he had been forgiven by the Heavenly Court for having slain the kohanim of Nov, and has been given place among the righteous.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on I Samuel 28:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/28:24:1)


**עֵגֶל מַרְבֵּק.** (תרגום:) ״עֵגֶל פָּטִים״:


**A fattened calf.** A fattened calf.



## Chapter 29


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on I Samuel 29:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/29:3:1)


**מִיּוֹם נָפְלוֹ.** מִיּוֹם חֲנָיָתוֹ עָלַי:


**From the day he settled.** From the day of his camping with me.¹

footnotes:
¹ See Bereishis 25:18, and Rashi there explains that נָפָל means ‘dwelt.’



## Chapter 30


### Verse 9

###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:9:1)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ עַד נַחַל הַבְּשׂוֹר.** שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת אִישׁ:


**And they came to the Nachal Besor.** [The] six hundred men.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:9:2)


**וְהַנּוֹתָרִים עָמָדוּ.** כְּשֶׁהִגִּיעוּ לְנַחַל הַבְּשׂוֹר, הָיוּ בָּהֶם עֲיֵפִים, וְנוֹתְרוּ שָׁם מִקְצָתָן:


**Some of them remained behind.** When they reached the Nachal Besor, there were among them some who were faint, and some of those remained there.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:10:1)


**אֲשֶׁר פִּגְּרוּ.** אֲשֶׁר נִמְנְעוּ, וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי לְשׁוֹן אֲרָמִי הוּא, תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל ״וְנֶהֶרְסוּ״ (יחזקאל ל:ד):


**Who were too exhausted.** Who refrained; and I say that this is Aramaic, the translation of וְנֶהֶרְסוּ¹ [=and they will be destroyed].

footnotes:
¹ Yechezkeil 30:4.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:14:1)


**נֶגֶב הַכְּרֵתִי.** לִדְרוֹם שֶׁל פְּלִשְׁתִּים, מָצִינוּ בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר פְּלִשְׁתִּים נִקְרָאִים גּוֹי כְּרֵתִים, ״יוֹשְׁבֵי חֶבֶל הַיָּם גּוֹי כְּרֵתִים״ (צפניה ב:ה):


**South of the Kerethite.** To the south of the Philistines. We find elsewhere the Philistines being called the כְּרֵתִים nation [in] "the inhabitants of the seacoast the nation of כְּרֵתִים."²

footnotes:
² Tzephanyoh 2:5.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:17:1)


**לְמָחֳרָתָם.** לְמוּדִים הֵם עֲמָלֵקִים לִלְקוֹת בְּיוֹם מָחָר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מָחָר אָנֹכִי נִצָּב״ (שמות יז:ט), לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר לְמָחֳרָתָם:


**Of the next day.** The Amaleikites are accustomed to being defeated on the second day [of battle], as it is stated, "tomorrow I will stand…"³ Therefore, it states, "on the(ir) next day."

footnotes:
³ Shemos 17:9.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:20:1)


**אֶת כָּל הַצֹּאן וְהַבָּקָר.** שֶׁשָּׁבָה הַגְּדוּד מִנֶּגֶב יְהוּדָה וּמִנֶּגֶב כָּלֵב:


**All the sheep and cattle.** Which the troop had captured from the south of Yehudah and from the south of Caleiv.


###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:20:2)


**נָהֲגוּ.** אֲנָשִׁים מַנְהִיגִים וְהוֹלְכִים לִפְנֵי הַמִּקְנֶה הַהוּא, לְהִתְכַּבֵּד וּלְהִתְהַלֵּל, זֶה שְׁלַל דָּוִד:


**They led.** Men who were leading and marching before those cattle, to honor and praise themselves,⁴ [by declaring,] 'This is Dovid's spoil.'

footnotes:
⁴ Some suggest that the men did this to publicize Dovid’s sovereignty.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:25:1)


**מֵהַיּוֹם הַהוּא וָמָעְלָה.** לֹא נֶאֱמַר וָהָלְאָה, אֶלָּא וָמָעְלָה, כְּבָר הִנְהִיג אַבְרָהָם בְּחֹק זֶה (בראשית יד:כד): ״בִּלְעָדַי רַק אֲשֶׁר אָכְלוּ הַנְּעָרִים וְגוֹ׳״, הֵם הַיּוֹשְׁבִים עַל הַכֵּלִים, ״וְחֵלֶק הָאֲנָשִׁים״, הַהוֹלְכִים בַּמִּלְחָמָה בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (מג ט):


**From that day on.** [Literally 'from that day and above']. It is not stated: "[from that day] on" but "[from that day] and above [=before]." Avrohom had already instituted this statute [when he said,] "Only what the lads have eaten,"⁵ those are the ones who stayed with the equipment, "and the portion of the men"⁶ who went to war. In Breishis Raboh.

footnotes:
⁵ Bereishis 14:24, where it is stated that Avrohom distributed shares of the spoils of war to those who stayed behind and guarded the equipment.
⁶ Ibid.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:26:1)


**לְרֵעֵהוּ.** לְאוֹהֲבָיו:


**To his allies.** To his friends.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on I Samuel 30:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/30:31:1)


**אֲשֶׁר הִתְהַלֶּךְ שָׁם דָּוִד.** שֶׁהָיוּ מַחְבְּאִים אוֹתוֹ מִפְּנֵי שָׁאוּל:


**Where Dovid [and his men] had gone.** For they would hide him from Shaul.



## Chapter 31


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on I Samuel 31:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:1:1)


**וּפְלִשְׁתִּים נִלְחָמִים בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל.** כְּאָדָם הָאוֹמֵר נַחֲזוֹר לְעִנְיָן רִאשׁוֹן:


**The Philistines were battling with Yisroel.** As a person saying, 'Let us go back to the earlier topic.'



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on I Samuel 31:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:11:1)


**וַיִּשְׁמְעוּ אֵלָיו.** כְּמוֹ עָלָיו, עַל שָׁאוּל:


**About him.** Meaning the same as עָלָיו [about him], i.e., about Shaul.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on I Samuel 31:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20I%20Samuel/r/31:12:1)


**וַיִּשְׂרְפוּ אוֹתָם שָׁם.** תַּרְגּוּם: ״וּקְלוֹ עֲלֵיהוֹן כְּמָא דְקָלָן עַל מַלְכַיָּא״ תַּמָּן, כִּדְתְנַן (עבודה זרה יא א) שׂוֹרְפִין עַל הַמְּלָכִים וְלֹא מִדַּרְכֵי הָאֱמוֹרִי:


**And burned them there.** [Targum renders,] "And they burned for them¹ as they burn for the kings there." As we learned:² We perform the burning [ritual] at [the burial of] kings, and it is not [considered] an Amorite custom.

footnotes:
¹ I.e., they burned incense to cover the odor and prevent the decay of the bodies.—Radak
² A king’s personal belongings are burned at his death to prevent other people from using them, as stated in Maseches Avoda Zarah 11a.
